# Decisions record

Live rulings for Tessalith 2.x. Three files hold the record:
- this file: the index, a digest of the 1.x rulings still in force, then the 2.x notes (Note FM first) in full;
- `.claude/DECISIONS-1.x.md`: the 1.x record, frozen at 2.0 (history only; its IDs stay citable);
- `.claude/DECISIONS-ARCHIVE.md`: superseded 2.x rulings.

A new note goes at the end of this file and adds its letter to the index line of every crate or area it governs. A letter is free only when none of the three files uses it.

## Digest

Rules still in force at 2.0, one or two lines each, with the 1.x IDs (in `.claude/DECISIONS-1.x.md`) or FM items they come from. Where a 1.x ruling and Note FM disagree, FM wins. The 1.x note holds the reasons and details; a cite is where to read them. `OPEN:` lines are promised work not done yet.

### Engine policy

- For texture artists converting source images to DDS for Skyrim SE/AE, not a game-folder DDS fixer; suffix inference proposes, the user decides category, encoder and settings. [Scope clarification]
- Windows only. [Answers 12]
- The product is Tessalith (GUI `tessalith.exe`); the CLI stays `sdc`. [F, AG1]
- BC4 and BC5 are unsigned; SE loads both legacy `BC4U`/`BC5U` FourCC and DX10 `BC4_UNORM`/`BC5_UNORM`. [Answers 2]
- BC6H (emissive, sky) works under Community Shaders only, never vanilla; `_SRGB` formats are not usable with vanilla shaders. [Answers 3, Answers 4]
- Any colour data in a PBR set is `_SRGB`-tagged (including `_g`, `_f`). [Answers 5]
- Two-channel BC5 is the default only for `_flow`; it stays selectable. [Answers 1]
- No small-texture restriction on BC7. [Answers 7]
- Normal length: renormalise, unless the set has an `_rmaos`/`_rfaos`, in which case the normal variance is baked into roughness. [Answers 6, R1, R6]
- Alpha that must read 255 is removed before any mip or encode step (alpha policy `remove`). Every BC7 block whose input alpha is 255 throughout gets the lossless rotated-slot fix, so bc7e decodes alpha ≥ 254. [Answers 8, A]
- CS treats smallest-mip alpha < 251/255 as complex material, so a decoded 254 is harmless. [A, BJ1]
- Source alpha with minimum ≥ 251, or with texels below 251 that are only stray noise, is opaque; it resolves like constant 255. An explicit `alpha.policy` still wins. One alpha gate, 251, matching CS. [L, BJ1, FM34]
- Cutout vs blend is a per-image choice; the alpha-test threshold is per texture (default 127, typical 5–200). Coverage preservation is on by default for Cutout and Cutout+Blend. [Answers 10, E]
- `_rfaos` = Roughness, Fuzz, AO, Specular: `_rmaos` with a fuzz mask in G; linear data, alpha = specular, same bake. [B]
- Intentional skips (existing `.dds` inputs, `--on-existing skip`, packing inputs) do not make the batch exit 1; unresolved, blocked, unknown existing outputs and failures do. Skips are still reported and counted. [I, M4]
- Hair diffuse is cutout + blend by default; a `multilayer` diffuse's alpha is data. [AK1, S-shader-5]
- Plain normals (`_n`, `_normal`, `_norm`) default to DirectX green; `IN-GREEN-UNSTATED` is Info; explicit settings win unchanged. [AW1, AW2, AW3, W13]
- A packed map cannot use an HDR format (BC6H and packing are mutually exclusive). [S12]
- `pbr_emissive` is BC6H only when the source is float and some finite RGB value exceeds 1.0; otherwise BC7 sRGB. `hdr_sky` is BC6H whatever the source. [O2, S3]
- A float source (float TIFF, EXR, HDR, float DDS) is linear light; Preprocess never sRGB-decodes it. [S1]
- OpenEXR alpha is un-premultiplied on load. [BQ15]
- `IN-NONFINITE` is reported once at plan time; statistics skip non-finite samples. [S9, BC6]
- An embedded ICC profile is ignored and not reported. [S8, FM21]
- Game-path keys (meshes, scan, BSA) fold ASCII only; set ids and `[files]` keys fold Unicode. [BF1]
- Files the user edits and DDS outputs are written with `engine::dds::write_atomic`, which flushes before the rename. [BC10, AV item 13]
- One rule for replacing an existing file; never overwrite a file Tessalith did not write. [BV3, BC2]
- The engine is MPL-2.0 (with `encoders-sys`); no commit may call it MIT. [DC41]
- The workspace version is the engine version; it follows semantic versioning (MAJOR breaks use, MINOR adds features, PATCH fixes). [DM8, DB1]
- Engine 2.0.0 has one encode version for every type; a 1.x record is a stale baseline and re-encodes once. [FM9, DM1]
- Removed config keys and values are errors that name the fix; the old-version upgrade code is gone, newer-version refusals stay. [FM10, FM13, T9]
- Fifteen info and low-value warning codes and the re-encode report are gone; anything the user must act on is a code with a fix. [FM21]
- An engine change that alters a type's bytes or verdict moves that type's arm in `encode_version` (Note DM); the byte-identity pins in `encode_versions/identity.json` are strict. [DM1, DM4, FM1]
- A new `Profile` field is `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = …)]` so existing profile hashes hold. [DM5, S-mips-9]
- `RELEASED_ENGINE_VERSION` is set by the release commit to the released version. [DM5b, FF8, FM41]
- Engine modules `coverage`, `dilate`, `migrate`, `pipeline` and `sysmem` are `pub(crate)`. [FA14]
- One set-base rule: `engine::group::base_of`. [FA13]
- `toml` 1 is the parser. [BL]
- Test-only hooks sit behind each crate's `test-hooks` feature (off by default, on through the crate's own dev-dependency, never in release); `sdc-gui`'s also forwards render3d, modscan and pgpsim `test-hooks`; render3d's hooks are `#[doc(hidden)]`. [K, FM27, GJ2]
- Dev and test builds keep line tables only. [FM2]
- The bench crate and raw tuning data live on local branch `bench`, not `main`. [FM8]
- NVTT is gone from the code; it is never bundled. [FM14]
- Cs and vanilla are the only targets; there is no `cpm` target. Complex material is a vanilla shader and complex grass is available under vanilla. [AZ5, AZ6]
- Planning order: pass 1 resolves every file, then each set's shader is picked; members of shaders with defaults or sub-type effects re-resolve; a panic is isolated per file; explicit shader picks are read before the packed-suffix prediction. `SHADER-*` warnings ride on `set_resolved`. `EVENT_SCHEMA` stays 2 while a change only adds fields or drops an enum value. [S-shader-7, AZ4, AW4]
- `Engine::plan_full` is `prepare` (setup, discovery, `[files]`, grouping, joins, typed roles, set edits, duplicates) then `plan_sets` (pass 1 onward); every edit still runs a full plan, which lands last and wins, and Export and Review run only on it. [FL2, FL9]
- `Need` is `subtype` or `alpha_meaning`; the green convention never makes a job Unresolved, so `unresolved_code` returns only `SUG-LOW-CONFIDENCE`. [AW4]
- Discovery keys each file by its canonical path: the first input to reach a file wins and a link and its target are one file. An unreadable entry under an input folder is `IN-UNREADABLE` (warning) and the batch goes on; an unreadable input root is an error. [BQ1, Y4]
- A game path cuts at the first `textures` component at or below the input root's last component, else the last one anywhere, else the relative path. A `textures` folder below the root wins over the root's own; a root named `textures` that holds mod folders keys from the mod's own `textures`. [BQ8]
- Glob errors by entry point: `--set file:<glob>` is a usage error, preset rule globs a preset error, config and alias-preset globs a config error; the compiled-matcher cache is lazy, never evicted. [BC5, Y4]
- A PNG decodes with a 64 MiB metadata budget (`load::PNG_METADATA_BUDGET`); other formats have none. Decoded pixels are not counted, so a 16384 px RGBA16 PNG still loads. [AV1]
- PSD/PSB are inputs (not Krita or OpenRaster): RGB or Grayscale, 8/16/32 bits; alpha is the document transparency (first alpha channel with a Background layer); a white-matted composite (negative layer count or `Mtrn`/`Mt16`/`Mt32`) is un-matted with `(c − (1 − a)) / a`. Bitmap, Indexed, CMYK, Multichannel, Duotone, Lab, no real composite or a ZIP composite is `IN-DECODE` with the reason; over 16384 px is `DIM-TOO-LARGE`. [AC2, AC3, AC4, FM23]
- `.exr`, `.hdr`, `.bmp` and `.jpg`/`.jpeg` load through the `image` crate; an undecodable file is `IN-DECODE`. [S10]
- Memory admission: a plan-pass cache hit admits at 0 bytes, a cold file costs one stat; a job over the budget runs alone when nothing else is in flight; a helper's decode bytes count beside its target's. [AV4, BB5, DY10e]
- A `_crn` makes a set PBR like `_cnr`. [BV9]

### Analysis and alpha

- The alpha question is one dropdown on the resolved type. [FM35]
- A file with no recognised suffix is the colour member, at High confidence; a lone one with an underscore waits for a type unless a scanned mesh or a GRAS model uses it as a diffuse (GY1, GY6). The GUI accepts High suggestions and records them in the project. [U1, U3, FC1, GY1, GY6]
- Mesh alpha evidence (NiAlphaProperty from the scan) decides a varying alpha's meaning through the `mesh:alpha` layer; the first plan asks until the scan evidence is read, then re-plans. [U5, U6, U7, U9]
- "(auto)" in the Sub-type row means the decision came from evidence, with the reason on hover. [U4]
- `_coat` is coat colour + strength (canonical `s`). [U2, S-shader-9]
- A complex `_m` without source alpha writes alpha 0; "Enable parallax" on its card keeps the height (off sets alpha 0). [AH1, AH2, M1]
- Alpha noise near 255 counts as opaque. [FM34]
- The BC3 alpha guard follows each texture's alpha-test cutoff. [FM19]
- Coverage (`VAL-COVERAGE`) runs on every mip level, also under `validation.mips = smallest`; PSNR then checks the last mip only. [T5, Y6]
- Artist checks run from analysis stats cached with the analysis; hints come from the canonical suffix; in the GUI they are one count, not amber, not ready signals, never Review (rows plus one end-of-export summary). [CL1, CL2, CL6, FC3, CL18, CL25]
- The analysis disk cache: `ANALYSIS_VERSION` alone versions it, the hints are part of every key, same size and mtime is "unchanged", a value that does not read back bit for bit is not stored, every I/O error is a miss, prune keeps the most recently written or used entries up to the caller's limit, the GUI's being a setting (Notes HR7, HV2). [DZ1, DZ3, DZ5, DZ6, DZ8, DZ9]
- Only `main.rs` reads `LOCALAPPDATA` for the analysis store; the GUI builds one `Session`. [DZ10, DZ11]
- A float (EXR) colour map is kept as stored values. [CN24]
- An Advanced-skin diffuse switches its set's packed RMAOS to RFAOS, both ways; a pinned diffuse is left alone. [DQ1, DQ3, DU, DW]
- The engine reads the chosen shader as Advanced-skin evidence; GUI and CLI agree; written values win. [DV, DW]
- A written plain `diffuse` on an Advanced Skin set reads as `skin_albedo`; any other written sub-type stays; a written `packing.recipe` decides the packed output alone. While the shader is Advanced Skin the diffuse cannot be plain `diffuse` (leave Advanced Skin for that). Vanilla is untouched. [DV3, DV1]
- A diffuse in a pinned slot turns the shader reading off for its set. The GUI sends locked slots' `[files]` keys as `BatchRequest::pinned_files` (a borrowing set's lock left out); the CLI sends none and never reads `sdc-project.toml`. `pack::plan::chosen_skin` is the one predicate. [DW1, DW2, DW3]
- A set that cannot build `_rfaos` (metallic-only sources, `PACK-RECIPE-UNBUILDABLE`) still takes the Advanced-skin sub-type and keeps `_rmaos`. [DQ2]
- Check stats ride in the cached `Analysis`: `AnalysisCache::checks` is off by default and on in `Session::new`; the CLI computes none, so CLI output, manifests and goldens do not change. [CL3]
- Green direction check: a Normal-category job other than `msn` whose blue mean is below 0.5 (not for BC5/RG8, which store no blue) raises `CHK-Z-SIGN`; as-stored curl above `CURL_FLOOR` (1e-4) with flipped curl under `GREEN_RATIO` (0.5) of it raises `CHK-GREEN-GUESS`, unless the profile already flips green. `CHK-GREEN-GUESS` is Info and never auto-flips. [CL7, CL10, CL8]
- Parallax check reads the `_p` in R (a complex `_m`'s A when parallax is on and the source has alpha) against the set's neutral: White for a Pbr set with multilayer and coat parallax on, none for vanilla Multilayer, else Half; it flags clipping over `CLIP_SHARE` 0.10 and steep steps over `STEEP_SHARE` 0.03. Landscape sets compare `displacement_scale`: a ratio over 2 (two sets) or more than 2× from the lower median (three or more) raises set-level `CHK-PARALLAX-SCALE`. [CL11, CL12, CL8, FM26]
- `CHK-LOW-DETAIL` (Warning) is the one CHK code the engine emits, measured during convert after coverage preservation, behind `BatchRequest::low_detail` (`Off` by default; never set by the CLI). Ratio: detail energy between mip 0 and nearest-up mip 1 over that between mips 1 and 2; below `LOW_DETAIL_RATIO` 0.25 flags. Eligible: `resize = none`, Color or Normal, not `complex_grass`, ≥ 3 levels, a power-of-two size `resize = half` would shrink above the floor; never a cube; bytes and records unchanged. [CL20, CL22, FM26, DS5]
- Under cs, hair has an optional suffix-only `_flow` role and an auto rule `flow` picks `hair` for a set with a `_flow` member on both targets, unless the path has a `water` folder; under vanilla a `_flow` still raises `SHADER-EXTRA-MAP`. [CL35, CL36]
- A complex-grass job has no base coverage and raises no `VAL-COVERAGE`: the top half is preserved in `grass::build` and the bottom half's alpha is specular data. [BC3, BC13]
- Complex grass has no alpha question (varying alpha: top half cutout, bottom half data); its set gets no `SHADER-EXTRA-MAP` / `SHADER-MISSING-MAP`; a grass stem ending in a raw-map suffix is read as that. [X12, X9, X8]
- A constant alpha-0 block of a `complex_material` with `alpha.parallax` off gets `encoders_sys::bc7_fixup::force_zero_alpha` after the encode (bc7e would otherwise decode alpha 1); BC3's alpha block stores 0 exactly. [BC7]
- The analysis cache admits with the load's hints, so an admission hit is a load hit; a disk entry without a thumbnail misses when thumbnails are wanted; the CLI caches nothing. `tests/analysis_version.rs` hashes generated PNGs, engine DDS files and `pack::assemble` results, so a loader change moves `ANALYSIS_VERSION`. [DZ2, DZ7, DZ4]

### Mips and resize

- The default mip filter is `box` for every sub-type and preset. [S-mips-1]
- Non-box filters need sRGB colour; tent, mitchell and kaiser levels are built directly from mip 0 with the kernel widened per level. [S-mips-2, S-mips-3]
- On alpha-weighted colour (`cutout`, `blend`, `cutout_blend`) the filter is box or tent; Mitchell and Kaiser are barred (they ring at cutout edges); HDR with alpha weighting takes box only. Weighted tent keeps the box alpha bit for bit. [FM28]
- Kaiser is fixed at width 3, α 4, stretch 1; Mitchell is B = C = 1/3, radius 2; tent radius 1. [S-mips-4]
- `mips.edge` = `wrap` (default) or `clamp`; only the polyphase kernels read it. [S-mips-5]
- An unscoped filter only affects colour maps, with a console message. [S-mips-13]
- Normal, `_msn`, `_flow` and roughness chains are never alpha-weighted. [R11]
- Normal mip 0 is renormalised by default (`mips.normal_base` = `renormalize` or `keep`). [R1]
- Power of two is automatic; `resize` is Half, Quarter or Eighth with a size floor, optionally relative to a slot of the set. [FM29, FM44]
- Pack sources of different sizes are resized to a common size. [FM30]
- `DIM-SMALL` and `DIM-FLOW-SMALL` test the post-resize size. [S-mips-7]
- Complex grass: each half is mipped on its own and the levels stacked; the detection texel is re-encoded to stay exact. [X1, X2]
- Resize runs at the end of `preprocess_with` (after the Hdr clamp; the pipeline and `BakeJob::load_lengths` both call it) with the effective mip filter and edge: box is area-exact, tent, Mitchell and Kaiser are polyphase on RGB; alpha is area box; alpha-weighted colour weighs area × alpha; roughness resizes on r²; normals, `_msn` and `_flow` resize as vectors, mip 0 renormalised unless `mips.normal_base = keep`. A growing axis is first enlarged bilinearly (texel centres, clamped edges); axes scale independently. [S-mips-6, W10, FM29]
- Coverage preservation passes exactly `k = round(p·n)` texels on every level ≥ 1 (level 0 too after a resize, Note HR6): those above the k-th largest alpha plus enough ties, split in a deterministic hierarchical Bayer order (no RNG; owner asked for the dither). `k = 0`, or a level with no positive alpha, keeps the box alpha; zero alpha is never raised. Remapped texels sit at least 2 codes clear of the threshold, saturating at the range ends (`alpha.threshold` 1–254). [R13]
- A hair diffuse's default layer sets `alpha.policy = cutout`, so a user's `mips.filter = kaiser` or `mitchell` on it is `CFG-INVALID` at sub-type or file scope and falls back to box with `CFG-FILTER-IGNORED` when unscoped. [S-mips-0, S-mips-2, S-mips-13]
- OPEN: the 13 GiB box peak at 16K and the single-threaded per-channel `powf` in Preprocess. [S-mips-11]
- OPEN: the complex-material gloss mip is the mean of gloss, not of α ("average α if banding shows"). [CL40]

### Encoders

- The format alone picks the encoder; the rgbcx level is fixed; the mode-6 byte patch is gone. [FM15]
- BC1 and the colour half of BC3 encode with rgbcx Dual (NVIDIA + AMD decoders); there is no decoder setting. [FM17, FM18]
- BC1 is opaque unless `encoder.bc1_alpha` is on (opt-in 1-bit alpha). [F, FM20]
- Perceptual BC1; vanilla colour is perceptual. [FM42, FM43]
- BC1 and RGB8 are allowed on a `data`-alpha type when the alpha is empty. [FM39]
- BC7 weighting is a dropdown with a stated precedence; inherited values are overridable; RMAOS weighting R4 G1 B2 A1 was dropped. [CP1, CP3, CP9, CP12]
- bc7e has an effort level `max`, off by default; the default stays byte-identical; level hashes are pinned per ISPC target; the endpoint search is not adopted in `veryslow`. [BZ1, BZ2, CB2, CB4]
- BC6H defaults to the peak-preserving mode (ISPC TC patch 0001); Slowest and Max use it. [FE3, FF1, FM43]
- BC4 block search: inner-anchored 6-value mode, least-squares refits, trimmed and local search, never worse per block; it carries a manifest tag. [FJ1, FJ2, FJ4]
- BC4/BC5 search radius as measured. [T11]
- The rgbcx rounding patch is applied. [T13]
- `encoder.bc7_weights` (the BC7 weighting dropdown) is optional and serialised only when set (existing profiles, presets, hashes and `sdc.toml` stay byte-identical). The weights reach bc7e only (other formats raise `ENC-WEIGHTS-IGNORED`); `encode_band` calls the tuned entry only with weights set and perceptual off, else the exact plain call; unit weights give the plain bytes. No preset, sub-type or rule sets it. [CP2, CP4, CP5, CP8]
- The GUI writes Linear as cleared fields; when an inherited perceptual or weighting is in effect it writes `perceptual = false` and `bc7_weights = "linear"` in the texture's own entry. [CP7, CP10]
- bc7e output depends on the CPU's ISPC target, so an AVX2 machine and an older one can write different BC7 bytes; the goldens hold on AVX2 machines. [CB3]
- Partition breadth without refinement is not a lever (bc7e refines only the winner; `m_refine_partitions` is part of `max`); `slowest` stays upstream's; per-channel normal weights are not adopted (they worsen Z, which Skyrim reads). Enabling the endpoint search (+0.05 dB colour at 1.4× time) would change every BC7 output, so it is an owner ruling. [BZ4, BZ3]
- The BC6H peak mode is a field value: on when a `bc6h_enc_settings` has both `slow_mode` and `fast_mode` (no upstream profile does), so no struct or ISPC header changes; the shim's `g_bc6[5]` is veryslow plus `fast_mode` (`Bc6hLevel::VeryslowPeak = 5`). `encoders-sys` `tests/bc6h.rs` pins the AVX2 output of the five levels; the owner accepted its +0.6 % RMSLE miss. [FE2, FE4, FE7]
- The complex-grass corner retry re-encodes at `Effort::Slowest` through `encode_band` (the LDR path, BC7 only), so it never reaches BC6H. [FF2]
- The BC3 coverage guard covers alpha only (BC4/BC5 are data). The new BC4/BC5 search also runs at radius 4 for the fast presets (`cs-compact`, `vanilla-compact`, `fast-preview`); it costs about 2.5× the old BC4/BC5 time at radius 4. The near-exhaustive `max` search was declined by the owner. [FJ7, FJ3, FJ8]
- Uncompressed outputs: `rgba8` (B8G8R8A8 UNORM/SRGB), `r8`, `rg8`, `rgba16f` (`cs` only). [O1, S4]
- Legacy uncompressed DDS: 24-bit RGB is read and written, A8L8 and L16 are read. [FM38]
- A `gray` sub-type: BC4 or R8, red by default, picked by hand; `channels.grayscale_source` picks R, G, B, A or luma. [FM36, FM37]
- OPEN: the non-AVX2 ISPC paths (SSE2/SSE4/AVX) of the BC6H peak mode were never hash-checked; `tests/bc6h.rs` skips without AVX2. [FF7]

### Packing and helpers

- Suffix vocabulary follows industry names; `_normalgl`/`_normaldx` resolve automatically. [H]
- Missing packed channels get neutral defaults plus a warning (AO white, metallic black, specular alpha white); roughness default 0.5. Fills are per packed output, `packing.fill.<role>`, 0–255. [H, M5, R-fills-2]
- `packing.recipe` = `auto`, `rmaos` or `rfaos`; `rfaos` without fuzz uses the fuzz fill. [R-fills-1]
- Advanced skin sub-types: `skin_albedo`, `skin_rf`, `skin_sk`, `skin_wet`, `skin_detail` (HB4). `rfaos` is not a sub-type id. [R-skin-1, R-review, FM11]
- A skin `_f` fills the RFAOS fuzz mask in a set already Advanced skin by other evidence; `_f` never makes a set Advanced skin by itself; a real `_fuzz`/`_sheen` wins G. [DR1, DR2, DR3, DR7]
- Env mask: a complex-material `_m` R is the metallic source, else specular, else the fill. [M1]
- Raw maps without a recipe raise `PACK-UNUSED-INPUT` and are not written; with two raw maps of one role the first by path is used. [M2, M13]
- Specular is copied unchanged into `_rmaos`/`_rfaos` A. [M3]
- Two-pass resolve: a packed output's `packing.*` comes from its profile resolved with a placeholder analysis. [M6]
- The packed suffix joins the set's suffixes before members resolve, predicted from the raw roles present. [M7]
- A packed job's input is its first source in R, G, B, A order; a raw map feeds at most one recipe per target. [M8]
- A pre-packed member whose canonical suffix equals the recipe's suffix is the pre-packed conflict (re-encoded DDS members included). [M9, BV1]
- A recipe with a failed source is `failed` and has no job. [M11]
- Packed provenance records each channel's source and channel (`:R`, `:G`, `:B`, `:A`, `:luma`, or the grayscale source). [M12, FM36]
- `_orm` is a packing input; loose maps win over it; `_rfaos` drops the ORM metallic. [AL1, AL2, AL5]
- Track A inputs: `_rmaos` by channel, `_curves` and cavity roles are packing inputs. [CN1, CN3, CN92]
- A raw map given a sub-type converts on its own, not as a packing role. [BP3]
- Memory admission counts complex grass. [BB4]
- Helper textures fill packed channels; hand roles have a fixed precedence; only images are helpers; role none (the GUI's unticked Helper box) opts a file out. [DY]
- Helper fills run before analysis; cubes and derived jobs never fill; a helper of another size is resized. [DY, FM30]
- `if-changed` sees a packed-channel move and the fill source (`SourceRef.fill`). [DY]
- Helpers are a collapsible group under the tiles, not a filter pill; helper rows take the keyboard. [FB12, FM31]
- `SET-MISSING-COMPANION` (info): a cs PBR set with `pbr_normal` but no roughness or the reverse, `_msn` without `_s` (not Eye), `_flow` without `_n`; `SET-COMPANION-DDS` (warning) when the companion is only a DDS. Neither on a `CFG-UNAVAILABLE` job or an albedo-only PBR set. [R8, BQ12]
- A usable `_n` is decided (Ready, or Blocked such as `FMT-BC5-NORMAL`); an Unresolved `_n` gives its members `SET-MISSING-COMPANION` ("not decided yet"); with two `pbr_normal` jobs in a GUI-built set the first by path is used. [R9]
- `_wet` is a suffix only on `cs`, in an Advanced skin set (a real `_rfaos` member or a resolved recipe building `_rfaos`): `group::join_skin_wet` moves `x_wet` into set `x` (after grouping, before set edits, folding Unicode); elsewhere `_wet` stays in the name (`road_wet`). `_detail_n` is a suffix in a skin set (`cs`) or in a landscape set on every target (a landscape path, or an explicit Landscape or PBR Landscape shader): `group::join_detail_n` moves `x_detail_n` into set `x`; in any other set `x_detail_n` groups as before (GW1). A set listing `wet` keeps a suffix-less `*_wet` member through a Set Rename. [I3, BP2, BV9]
- Skin detection uses the recipe actually chosen (a forced `rmaos` with fuzz is not skin). RFAOS is offered only where buildable (roughness, glossiness or AO source); `PACK-RECIPE-UNBUILDABLE` is attached only when `auto` would not build `_rfaos`. [I1, I2, BQ13]
- A failed or cancelled skin `_f` fails the `_rfaos` recipe (no default G): `pack::plan::skin_f_candidate` is the one predicate, `is_skin_f` adds "no requested sub-type", and `plan_packing` takes a role from `JobStatus::PackingInput { role }` when the name has none. A skin set writes no `_f.dds`; an old one is not removed. [DR4, DR5, DR8, DR9]
- An `_orm` source adds an optional `channel` (0..=2) to `ChannelSource::Source`, serialised only when set (other jobs keep their bytes); provenance reads `iron_orm.png:G`. `RawMap::ALL` stays the eight single-channel fill roles (a fill for `orm` is refused). `_curvature` and `_curves` read as `curves`, `_cavity` as `cavity`. [AL3, CN2]
- A helper is set per file in its `[files]` entry (or `--set file:<glob>.helper.<field>=`): `helper.role`, `helper.fills` (target ids; `[]` = unused), `helper.read` (`r`, `g`, `b`, `a`, `luma`), `helper.invert`. A `subtype:` scope, an unscoped `--set helper.*` and `helper.*` in rules or presets are refused. [DY1, DY1a, DY1b]
- A hand-set `helper.role` (images only) makes the file a helper even when a rule or `[files]` names its sub-type; `none` converts it alone. Otherwise a helper's role is its own `PackingInput` job's; a raw map typed to convert alone fills nothing. A hand-role file with no known suffix joins its base set in the same output folder. [DY2, DY2a, DY13a, DY20]
- Alpha fill targets: `diffuse.a` (Diffuse, PbrAlbedo, SkinAlbedo), `n.a`, `f.a` (PbrFuzz), `s.a` (PbrSubsurface), `cnr.a`; the first matching job by input. A suggested fill applies only to empty source alpha (none, 255, or min == max; else "already has alpha"); an assigned fill always replaces. Assigned beats suggested, then first by path; an assigned fill without facts reserves its target; alpha fills stay active with `packing.enabled = false`. [DY6, DY6a, DY7, DY9, DY9a, DY10a]
- Unset `helper.read` is R (luma where the recipe says; never A automatically); on an `_orm` it overrides each slot's channel. `helper.invert` flips on top of the recipe. `packing.fill.*` applies only when no helper targets the channel. A hand-built recipe keeps the set's suggested sources elsewhere and honours `packing.recipe`; a hand `rfaos.*` fill builds `_rfaos` on an Advanced Skin set. [DY5, DY5a, DY17g, DY12, DY4a, DY4c, DY23]
- Fill step order: pass 1, alpha facts, `plan_cubes`, `plan_fills`, `plan_packing`, pass 2, fills, derive. Each target loads with `load_image_as` and is re-analysed; a load failure removes its job; pass-1 DDS-format flags are kept; a consumed helper whose target is gone ends with that target's output. [DY10, DY10b, DY10c, DY10d]
- `Plan.helpers` holds one `HelperReport` per helper (by set key, then file name) and the GUI renders and edits only it. `FuzzWeight` and `SubsurfaceWeight` come only from a hand-set role. [DY13, DY3]

### Bake

- The roughness member decodes its own `_n` and bakes its variance (clamp t = 0.25); footprint lengths are a box pyramid; a pair whose sizes do not line up (not powers of two of one aspect) bakes from the `_n` fitted to the member's size (Note HD2), so no size pair is refused. [E, R4, R5, HD2]
- The bake decision lives on the `_n` (`renormalize_bake`); shared roughness never bakes; a borrowed `_n` bakes the borrower's own roughness. [R6, R7, M4-final-1]
- `_cnr` self-bakes its variance into coat roughness by default. [R2]
- An 8-bit noise floor: a footprint with average length ≥ the floor bakes nothing. [M4-final-2]
- sRGB roughness raises `TAG-CONTENT-MISMATCH` and does not bake. [R10]
- The baked `_n` is recorded in the manifest sources with its decode settings, so `if-changed` sees it. [R12]
- A generated map (height, AO or curvature made elsewhere) is recognised by its `_generated` name marker only, and a real map for the slot wins over it (`GEN-REPLACED`, an intentional skip). [CO1, CO2, CO5, CO9]
- A generated `_p` writes the plain `_p` output; a preset alias may not be `generated`. [CO3, CO4]
- An `_ao_generated` file feeds the packed `_rmaos` B (`GEN-AO`), also on a pre-packed `_rmaos` whose B holds no content. [CO89, CO103, CO104]
- The GUI's own Export refuses an export folder equal to the opened folder. [CO75]
- A short normal gets one Z warning, from the engine, with a Rebuild Z fix. [DP4, DP5]
- BC5/RG8 normal sources rebuild Z. [BP4]
- Opt-in gloss bake for a complex `_m`. [CL40]
- A generated map rivals a real map only until the real one appears: step 3b (after `apply_output_paths`, before duplicate detection) drops the loser from `set.members` with no job, skipped `GEN-REPLACED` (Info) "generated <name> replaced by <real name>". The generated member's own earlier export (`dds::same_file`, also its packed outputs: the member's output folder joined with the rival's name) is no rival, so an in-place run keeps it. A replaced member never reaches the pre-packed conflict, so no `PACK-CONFLICT`. [CO6, CO7, CO8, CO10, CO102]
- A kept `_ao_generated` stays PBR evidence (`suggest::is_pbr_set`). A pre-packed `_rmaos` counts as an AO source only when its B has content (`pack::b_has_content`: range ≥ 2/255 or mean ≤ 250/255). A loose roughness, glossiness, metallic, specular, fuzz or `_orm` image beside a pre-packed one means the image is never filled, except a specular beside a flat alpha (HC1). [CO92, CO94, CO105, CO106, CO108]
- Curvature (`_curves`, `_curvature`, `_curves_generated`) is read only by Also export, and reaches a plain run as `PACK-UNUSED-INPUT`. [CO116, CO115]

### Cubemaps

- Cube maths lives in the engine; separate cube entry points, no `DdsDesc.cube` field. [DS1, DS2]
- `Subtype::Cubemap` with a `cubemap` section and styles; an auto face below 4 is `CFG-INVALID`; the auto face size stops at 4096. [DS4, DS20, DS21]
- Raw-face layouts and cube DDS input; one rule for "under the cubemaps folder". [DS7, DS23]
- Cube transfer is Community Shaders' game gamma. [DS11]
- The world-to-cube basis comes from the in-game test; yaw and mirror turn a cube. [DS13, DS14]
- The preview's panorama mapping runs clockwise, like a 360° photo. [DL1]
- A 2D `_e` file is a cube decision, not a 1.x warning; `--strict-cubemaps` is gone. [FM22, FC7]
- GUI: `Need::CubeLayout`; a cube preview encodes all six faces; the cross preview shows them and obeys the 2D preview cap; a six-file cube is named `<output stem> (6 faces)` and its edits go to all six face entries; its size estimate is six faces at `face_size`. [DT1, DT2, DT5, DT6, DT16, DT22, DT31]
- A cube is not a set member; Cubemap sits in the "Other" group of the sub-type dropdown. [DT4, DT24]
- Make marker cube… lives in the Export to ▾ menu; its dialog is modal with the Enter default on Save…; roughness defaults to 0.5 when ticked. [DT27, DT28, DT30]
- Cube encodings are BC1, BC3, BC7 or RGBA8 UNORM, else `CFG-INVALID`; a cube skips `DIM-NPOT`/`DIM-SMALL`; `mips.levels = none` (or 0, 1) warns `CUBE-NO-MIPS`. Ranges: `face_size` power of two 4..=4096, `face_rotate` 0/90/180/270, `blur` 0..=1, `exposure` −10..=10, `brightness` and `contrast` −1..=1, `midtones` 0.1..=10, `saturation` 0..=2, `highlight_clamp` ≥ 1. [DS5, DS20, DS21]
- `face_tag` reads a trailing `_posx` .. `_negz` or `_px` .. `_nz`; `x_e_posx` splits as base `x`, suffix `e`, so the six faces of an `_e` map join set `x` (any other face-tagged stem is untouched: `rock_px` is its own set). The merged job keeps the +X face's set key. `plan_cubes` runs after pass 1 and the 6c re-resolve, before `plan_packing`. [DS8]
- A cube job skips the 2D stages: each source loads and preprocesses with `resize = none` (a cube is sized only by `cubemap.face_size`); a source count other than 1 or 6, or faces that are not equal squares, fail the file at Load. The display-space filter skips any step at its default, and `yaw` and `blur` run only when non-zero, so default settings are bit exact. [DS10, DS9, DS24]
- A cube DDS loads as its six faces, an h-strip: `analysis.dds_cube` wins over a face tag in the name and over `cubemap.layout`, and an explicit layout other than hstrip is `CFG-INVALID`. Admission sees the real 6w × h strip. [DN7a]
- The cube section lists the 16 `cubemap.*` rows from `fields::CUBE_FIELDS`, shows only when every selected item is a cube, and shows out-of-range values rather than fixing them (each drag value is bounded to the engine's range). Ticking Highlight clamp writes 4.0 and Blur 0.35. [DT7, DT8, DT9, DT10]
- The planned layout wins over the setting; a six-file set shows "Six face files" and the layout combo never offers it. A six-file cube's source pane has six buttons, +X to −Z, that show the processed face (the same image as its cross cell); a one-file cube's source pane shows its input image. [DT11, DT13, DT20, DT21]
- A cube draws with Tile 3x3 off without touching the saved setting (the mask is applied where it is read), and Heatmap is not offered for a cube ("Not for cubes"). [DT17, DT23]
- Make marker cube…: a Kept (a file Tessalith did not write) or Failed write leaves the dialog open with the note inside it; closing the dialog cancels a Save still open, so a path picked afterwards writes nothing. [DT29, DT33, DT34]
- OPEN: static cubes use a Gaussian cone, not GGX. [DS]
- OPEN: no Reload offer for cube faces 1-5 or the bake's `_n` on their own row. [FA3]

### Presets and config

- Presets declare exact suffix aliases (no separator): an alias binds a filename suffix to a canonical suffix for grouping and output naming and may set any preset field; an alias is any declared name. [G]
- Built-in preset ids: `sdc-base`, `cs-quality`, `cs-compact`, `vanilla-quality`, `vanilla-compact`, `fast-preview`. [T1]
- Every matching rule of a source layers its overrides (preset chain, user config, project config), not first match. [T14, AP10]
- Duplicate preset ids are refused, naming both sources (`CFG-PRESET-SKIPPED` in the folder, an error from `--preset-file`). [T7]
- Presets and configs are at schema 1 with no migration steps; newer versions are refused. [T9, FM13]
- A preset's sub-type settings are the "no suffix" default; a suffix branch is one rule after the others, recognised by shape; remaps live in the branch. [AP1, AP2, AP3, AP6]
- The preset creator has two families, PBR / Community Shaders then Vanilla (`family_of`: cs only is PBR / CS, else Vanilla; `complex_grass` placed in PBR / CS by hand); the Target dropdown lists base, cs, vanilla. [AP9, AP12, AZ6]
- The preset creator edits the preset's TOML table as a draft, validated through the engine loader; broken preset files show in the window; unsaved edits ask first. [AM3, AM4, AM15, AM16]
- A built-in preset views without an error and offers only Duplicate. [FB6]
- A preset save may not break another user preset. [BR9]
- Per-texture GUI edits are `[files."<path relative to sdc.toml>"]` entries (any profile field plus `allow = [codes]`), their own layer. [N2]
- A quoted dotted `[files]` key is a config error; the GUI finds an extension-less `[files]` key; `[files]` matching runs once per file through an index. [CV4, CV19, AV3, FL1]
- `[export]` in `sdc.toml` is project-only; parse and check are separate. [CN28, CN29, CN30]
- A clean `sdc.toml` is never written; a hand edit is taken. [AE3]
- The `cpm` target is gone: naming it is a hard error. [AZ1]
- `--set set:<id>.shader=<shader>` is the only set-scope form; `shader = "<id>"` sits in the set's `[[sets]]` entry; the shader layer applies after presets, configs and rules. [S-shader-1, S-shader-2, S-shader-3]
- Shader sub-type effects: Eye `_n` becomes `msn`, a landscape colour member becomes `landscape_diffuse`. [S-shader-4]
- Shader auto-pick order: explicit, advanced skin, PBR, complex `_m`, `_p`, mesh majority; the map wins over the mesh. [S-shader-10, S-shader-11]
- The PBR rule is target-independent: a PBR set under vanilla auto-picks `pbr` and falls back to `default` with `SHADER-UNAVAILABLE`. [S-shader-8]
- `_i` suggests `diffuse`; rule 12 picks `default`, never `pbr` without a PBR map. [S-shader-6]
- A rule's `overrides` may not set `subtype`: `check_preset` and `check_up_front` refuse it ("set the sub-type with `subtype =` on the rule"). [BC11]
- `presets::check_chain` is the one rule check (glob compiles, known field paths, no `overrides.subtype`, per-sub-type profile builds) over the whole chain; a rule override path outside `FIELD_PATHS` (`target`, `category`) is refused in presets and configs. Every glob compiles where it enters (`policy::compile_glob`), so a typo is a usage or config error. [BJ2, BC5]
- The config schema's `FileEntry` carries `reencode` (boolean); a test walks every `FIELD_PATHS` path except `subtype` through it. [BC8]
- A suffix alias naming exporter vocabulary is `CFG-INVALID`: `orm` (message "orm is a built-in packing input"), `curves`, `curvature`, and `cavity`. [AL6, CN4, BV9]
- User presets load from `%APPDATA%\sdc\presets\*.toml` (non-recursive, file-name order; `BatchRequest::user_presets`, `None` in tests). All load before checking, so a base may come later; a refused file is skipped with `CFG-PRESET-SKIPPED` and `add_dir` retries while checks keep removing broken presets; a file reached twice loads once. [T6, BQ2]
- Preset merge details: a `null` patch never adds a key (`merge_json`); a rule whose `subtype` is not the final sub-type does not apply (also after a `CFG-UNAVAILABLE` fallback); explicit `flip_green` wins over `CFG-GREEN-CONFLICT`; `hdr_sky` applies only under a `sky` component. [T10]
- `RuleMatch` has `match.subtypes` (used while layering, never while deciding the sub-type) and `match.alpha` (`none`, `constant255`); no matcher reads a resolved policy, since that would be circular. [T2]
- Compact presets: opaque colour becomes BC1; an explicit non-`remove` alpha policy on an opaque colour map under compact is `FMT-BC1-ALPHA`; `landscape_diffuse` is never BC1 (`FMT-LANDSCAPE-BC1`); `pbr_subsurface` and `pbr_fuzz` are BC1 only without alpha; `pbr_emissive` and `hdr_sky` stay full size and `hdr_sky` is excluded from resize. [T2, T3]
- Preset names are the id and file name: lower-case letters, digits and `-`, not starting with `-`, checked live. Duplicate copies as `<id>-copy` (then `-copy2`) without `suffix_aliases` and their remap rules; New starts on the GUI target's quality preset; built-ins are read-only with Duplicate. Rows group Colour, Normal, Data, Special in `Subtype::ALL` order; a new alias is checked by loading a probe preset (Add only when it passes); a sub-type with no §6.1 suffix takes no alias. [AM5, AM6, AM7, AM9, AM11, AM13, AP7]
- A user preset saves to the file it came from (name need not match id), edited in place with `toml_edit` (unchanged keys keep text, comments, order), atomically; a new one goes to `<folder>/<id>.toml`, refused if it exists. Save or delete rescans and re-plans; Delete (user presets only) asks; selecting a preset rescans. Reset preset drops `[subtypes]` and every branch rule. The folder is `%APPDATA%\sdc\presets`, `None` or a temp folder in tests; the TOML preview shows what Save writes. [AM8, AM11, AM13, BO3, AP5]
- A channel remap (Data rows only) is a suffix alias plus a rule `match = { glob = "*_<alias>.*" }`, `overrides = { channels = { swizzle = "<4 of r,g,b,a,0,1>" } }`; removing the alias removes the rule. Branch rules are recognised by shape (only `match` and `overrides`, `match` only `subtypes` plus `suffix` or that glob), so a hand-written one is adopted; other rules are read-only and kept after the branches. The `rmaos` row shows ORM → RMAOS as text. Packing fills are `[subtypes.<id>.packing.fill]` keys. [AM10, AP3, AP4]
- The header's Preset combo lists "default (<the Mode's quality preset>)" and every selectable preset whose chain target is the Mode's or unset; a pick writes `preset = "<id>"` (default removes it) as one undo step. A preset id gone from disk is left out of the plan (the user config's or the Mode's default applies); a red Project row "Preset {id} is not installed" with Choose preset… gates Review; `sdc.toml` keeps the name; the CLI errors. [AM12, AM14]
- A complex `_m` packed under vanilla needs the set's shader `complex_material` (explicit, `--set set:<id>.shader` or auto: a member typed `complex_material` and no raw map starting an `_rmaos`); `cs` ignores it. The recipe id stays `cpm_m` (`RecipeKind::CpmM`). Under vanilla `Shader::available` is the old cpm list (11 shaders) and `complex_grass` auto-tags; `policy::offered` hides `complex_grass` from the sub-type pickers. [AZ2, AZ3, AZ5]
- The map check gives a `complex_grass` set no `SHADER-EXTRA-MAP`/`SHADER-MISSING-MAP` (one file holds diffuse and normal), and raises no `_msn` without `_s` note when the set's shader is Eye. [X9, BQ12]
- PBR grass under `textures/landscape/` is PBR: for a `pbr_grass` set `member_subtype` maps `landscape_diffuse` → `pbr_albedo`, `landscape_normal` → `pbr_normal`. A PBR set with a member in `BatchRequest::gras_all_diffuses` (CLI sends none) auto-picks `pbr_grass`; an explicit choice wins; a failed scan read keeps the old grass evidence. [BV5, AS2]

### Manifest and outputs

- Provenance is a batch manifest. [F]
- Every per-file event carries the job key (output path relative to the output root). Skip kinds are fixed. [N5, N6]
- Manifest sources record role and invert; the manifest compacts. [N7, BJ4]
- `ManifestRecord.encode_version` is an `Option`; the guard hashes the profile and sources (with stated blind spots). [DM3, DM4]
- One output path, one job; an explicit packed output path is checked for duplicates across every recipe that writes. [BV2, AE4, AF7]
- Derive identity carries no paths: the per-source SHA-256s and roles in slot order identify the job, so moving a project re-encodes nothing. [CT1, CT2]
- A filled output records its helper last as a source with `fill` (`a:<r|g|b|a|luma>`, plus `:inv`); a packed source records `read:<id>` for an explicit `helper.read`, then `ch:<rgba>`. `process_file` and `api::current_sources` both call `pack::source_fill`, so a changed helper, read or invert re-encodes; outputs without a helper record no `fill`. [DY14, DY21, FM12]
- A packed source carries an optional `channel` (`ChannelSource::Source`, serialised only when set); provenance reads `iron_orm.png:G`; the `orm` role is not in `RawMap::ALL` (no `packing.fill` for it). [AL3]
- `Manifest::read` is `load` without the compaction rewrite: readers that write nothing (the `IN-DDS-SKIPPED` summary, a dry run, `inspect`) use it; the converting load still compacts under the append lock. The `if-changed` checks run on the batch pool and skip events stay in plan order. [BQ6, Y3]
- A copied Vanilla output (Also export) is the PBR twin's record with `output` = the Vanilla key, its own `output_stamp`, and `copied_from` = the twin's `output_stamp` (skipped when `None`). It is current when `sources` and `source_sha256` equal the twin's, `copied_from` equals the twin file's stamp now and `output_stamp` matches the file. An existing file under `if-changed` goes through `may_replace` with the Vanilla record's stamp as "ours"; `Kept` is `OUT-UNKNOWN-EXISTING`. [CN33]
- A derived job's identity is in the manifest; its analysis is not cached. [CN12, CN22]
- `packing` joins every `Profile`. [M10]
- JSON hashes are recorded at write time. [FC11]
- The manifest-compatibility shims are gone. [FM12]
- Uncompressed output layouts and DDS re-encode: `reencode` is a plan-time selector; mip 0 is decoded, the rest regenerated. [S5, S6]

### Exports

- PGPatcher JSON: engine takes explicit output paths per source; slots are PGPatcher's 1-based `slotN` (`_cnr`/`_f` share slot 7, `_s` is slot 8); borrowed slot paths are written with `textures\`. [P1, P6, P11]
- The PGPatcher `default` block is fixed; colours are stored 0–255 and written as 0–1 with 4 decimals; `emissive`, `parallax`, `subsurface` are written only as `true`. [P10, P16, P design conversation]
- Glint and fuzz switches are GUI-only settings keys, never in the JSON; fuzz and coat normal grey each other out. [P17, P23]
- JSON paths that never match, and invalid path characters, block export; a JSON-only edit does not re-plan. [P7, P13, P26]
- Export refuses a plan that does not match the current sets; the JSON name is a single file name. [P20]
- A read-only `sdc-project.toml` still gets its JSON written, with the hash in `_sdc/generated.json`. [P21]
- One match key per entry; a set renamed to a file name with one match row and no `path_contains` carries `match_diffuse` + `rename` only; any other rename keeps its matches and names its files in slot commands (Note HJ3). [AN1, AN2, AA8]
- Right-click Lock is PGPatcher's slot lock; the sdc slot lock is "Pin this slot". [AN3, AN4]
- A set with no active match (only a typed key can leave none: `match1 = ""` and no other) shows a Warning row and is left out of the JSON. [V4, HL6]
- PGPatcher JSON is GUI-only, so GUI and CLI agree for non-PBR sets only. A texture-set block's `statics = "auto"` takes the non-LOD winning meshes of the set's key from the scan (no scan, no entry; a diffuse no mesh uses yet still writes the entry, with an Info row, HL6); a PBR Landscape set uses its card's box. [P14, AD7]
- A `match_diffuse` that is a strict path suffix of a diffuse in another folder is a pitfall (Proceed anyway; needs a scan). Lock keys are `lock_<slot>` for slots 1-4, 6-8; `lock_cubemap` clears slot 5 and no card offers it. Delete hits compare texture paths only; a `nif_filter` is reported, not checked. [AF8, AN5, AB3]
- Paths in the JSON editor are stored with `\` (a typed `/` in a match still blocks), as are `path_contains` and `nif_filter`. An exact-slot match with no known path is a pending pill until a path is typed or picked. A slot link writes the output's file name in its game-path folder; own known-texture rows have no extension. [BY4, BY5, BY7, BY8, CV8, CV9]
- A case-only rename removes the old-case JSON twin only when a hash record exists and the twin still hashes to it (`history::remove_own_case_twin`; CS PBR and complex-material exports both use it) and the `.dds` exists; a hand-edited or unrecorded twin is kept with a note and its record stays under the old key. [BO4, BY10]
- An Export clicked during a scan-finished database read starts when the read lands; only a user edit (undo and redo included) cancels it, with a message. A queued export never starts over an open dialog, there is no end-of-export JSON question, and the end-of-export error never replaces an open dialog. [AF4, AF9, AF10, AF11, BV10]
- Set ids, the set prefix and the input-name auto id use the export folder the current sets were planned with (`AppModel::sets_root`), so a new export folder picked before the re-plan lands does not corrupt keys. [BV8]
- Community Shaders PBR files: one file per EDID, keys sorted as CS's own saver writes them, 4-space indent, every number a float, `\n` line ends, no final newline; a material object always has `specularLevel` (0.04 when unset). A template applied to a block with no EDIDs adds the template's EDIDs in the same undo step; a block with EDIDs keeps them. [AD3, AD4]
- PGPatcher complex-material JSON is `<job output>.json` under the export root, validated against the vendored schema, written after an export that was not cancelled and whose own phase had no failed file (FW1: with Also, the CM phase, so a failed Vanilla file does not hold it back, and a failed CM phase skips it with a note naming CM); problems get a note. No `_m.json` importer; an `_m.json` Tessalith did not write is never overwritten; `disabled` round-trips. [AT3, GE13, FW1]
- XPMF: `editorIds` defaults to the set's `cs_mato` EDIDs as exact names (editable, wildcards allowed); with none, nothing is written and a note says so. `pbr` follows the set; with it on, a listed EDID no `cs_mato` block names gets an amber row and `specularMult` is hidden and omitted. Texture keys default to the set's outputs; LOD keys are optional. The picker offers MATO records for `editorIds` and `excludeEditorIds` only. [GI3, GI4, GI5, GI6, GI9, GI24]
- A MATO-only landscape set needs no RMAOS. CS does not draw the projected normal and detail samples in this build (slot hover says so); they still export. A landscape `_noise` is always ProjectedNoise; the detail names are `detail_normal` there (GW1). The two new kinds have no slot number, so no slot-keyed map gets them. The card shows every refusal `prepare` can know. The preview takes the profile's textures, coverage, then MATO DATA, cycling the layer when texture paths change. [GI10, GI15, GI16, GI17, GI18, GI19, GI21, GI25, GI28, GI29]
- Delete sections are `[[pgp_sections]]` with `delete: true`, written after every set's entry, only with a texture match; alone they never write the JSON. [AB2, AF1]
- JSON hash records name their file, one per file name. [BV7, BR6]
- Advanced skin sets get no PGPatcher JSON; they get a `.csskin.json` instead (HB1). [AG90]
- Community Shaders PBR texture sets and material objects: blocks from one source, guarded by the recorded hash, refusals per file. [AD1, AD2, AD5, AD6]
- PGPatcher complex-material JSON (`[sets.cm]`); vanilla keeps a complex `_m`. [AT1, AT2]
- XPMF profiles: every schema key in the block, one file per set named from the set id, the vendored schema checked before every write, null or empty optional values omitted, an `xpmf_sha256` record in a writable `sdc-project.toml`. [GI1, GI2, GI7, GI8, GI23]
- Landscape sets have two optional maps, projected noise (BC4_UNORM, `noise` is a global suffix) and the detail normal as its own output, named `_detail`, `_detailnorm` or `_detail_n` (GW1). [GI12, GI13, GI14, GI20]
- Also export (PBR to Vanilla and CM): three folders, three runs; derives only from the Pbr, PbrLandscape and PbrGrass sets the cs PBR export converts, skipping a set with any DDS member; copies before the Vanilla run; a later-phase error keeps its class; no zip, sums or conversion report. [CN32, CN41, CN42, CN99, FM23, CO116]
- Also export with Vanilla ticked puts a PBR Landscape set's projected noise, detail normal and a `pbr = false` XPMF profile in the Vanilla folder. [GI27]
- Derive: diffuse chain clamped before the soft light, non-metal env into the CM `_m`, landscape and grass rules, one terminal event per member. [CN6, CN7, CN10, CN26, CN27]
- `[export.legacy]` recipe defaults: brightness 0.9, ao_strength 0.5 (landscape 0.3), curves_opacity 0.40, metal_darken 0.3, spec_scale 0.66, spec_metal_boost 2.0, landscape_spec 2.5, cm_dielectric_env 0, keep_metal_brightness off (strength 1.0); a value out of range is exit 2 naming its key, checked only with `also`. The diffuse chain is k, AO, clamp, soft-light `_curves`, cavity, metal factor, clamp, in f32. [CN5, CN60, CN6]
- A derived CM `_m` has G = `max(1 - roughness, 5/255)` and A = the `_p` R clamped to [5/255, 250/255] (0 with no `_p`, so parallax stays off); env `_m` R = `gloss * max(metal, cm_dielectric_env)`. Toksvig is not applied to a derived CM `_m`. [CN9, CN7, CN19]
- Derived jobs are their own kind (`vanilla_diffuse`, `cm_diffuse`, `legacy_n`, `cm_m`, `env_m`, `grass_stack`), decided with provenance `recipe:<kind>`, reading their source's `[files]` entries; a diffuse whose albedo alpha varies waits for its meaning. Sources must share one aspect ratio (else `PACK-SIZE-MISMATCH`); float-ness comes from the colour map. [CN11, CN19, CN16]
- A `grass_stack` whose alpha policy would be `remove` becomes `cutout` (provenance `recipe:grass_stack`), even over an explicit `remove`, because the bottom half's alpha is the specular mask. [CN14, CN21]
- Derive objects: Vanilla writes `vanilla_diffuse` (albedo), `legacy_n` (`_n`), and `env_m` only for a real metal source on a mesh that votes Environment Map; CM writes `cm_m`, plus `cm_diffuse` with Keep metal brightness on. Each deriving set gets a `Derived` shader pick with no `SHADER-UNAVAILABLE`; the `_m` jobs list the albedo first so an albedo edit re-encodes them. [CN17]
- `vanilla_rel` removes the path component mirroring the `pbr` folder directly below `textures/` (from the end, ASCII case-insensitive) from the output; a derived job's `InputFile` keeps `rel` for its `[files]` key. A derived output landing on another job's output is `DERIVE-PATH-CONFLICT`, keyed by the member's own unfolded output. [CN18, CN97]
- The derive step removes every job of a derived or dropped set; a needed DDS source is `DERIVE-DDS-SOURCE` (intentional), no albedo and no `_n` is `DERIVE-NO-ALBEDO` (exit 1). Every member ends with one terminal event (`packed_source` skip or `DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED`); a landscape `_p` is dropped when its set derived nothing. [CN16, CN23, CN98]
- Also folder names: configured, else `"{project} PBR"`, `Vanilla`, `CM`; an empty, separator-holding, dot/space-ending, Windows-illegal or reserved-device name is refused naming its key, as are names equal after case folding. None of the three folders may equal or contain an input folder; a location containing the opened folder is fine. [CN31, CN36, CN37, CV12]
- The Vanilla and CM phases are the batch request with `target = vanilla`, their own folder and no low-detail check; the preset passes through, else `vanilla-quality`. A cs-only `--set` fails preflight (exit 2); a non-`cs` PBR plan is refused. Each phase has its own job-key prefix and batch id; batch-level warnings come once, from the PBR phase; both phases plan through the batch's cache. [CN34, CN35, CN44, CN66, CN70]
- Copy rule: a ready, non-derived, non-packed Vanilla job whose cs twin has the same identity (`also::same_identity`) and is current is copied from the PBR folder, not encoded; a failed or stale PBR output is never copied. A PBR set in `[export] skip` is dropped with a note. A CM-only derived plan error fails in the CM phase (exit 1). [CN33, CN40, CN55]
- The GUI Also export has no dialog: with a tick on, Export goes through Review, whose Export starts it. Ticks and settings are `[export]` edits (non-default fields only; explicit pins kept), one undo step each, refused while an export runs; ticking needs cs mode. With a tick on the PBR folder is the output root and the location is not a source. Review's gate order is FM24's; the Also gate checks sdc.toml writable, cs mode, the location, `ExportConfig::check()` and the names. Recipe edits write once per drag; renaming a set moves its `[export] skip` entry. [CN61, CN62, CN63, CN69, CN73, CN76, CN79, CN96, FM24]
- Files an earlier export left are always reported. [CN94]
- Vanilla/CPM borrowed slots are file copies, not tracked by the manifest; skipped with a note when the owner's output did not export. [P4, P22]
- Krita and OpenRaster files are not read; PSD/PSB merged image and WebP are. [AC1, AC7, FM23]
- OPEN: the CLI marks no inner-layer `_p`; a packed job is never copied under Also export. [CN]

### CLI

- `inspect`, `validate` and `presets` print their report on stdout. [J]
- The Ctrl-C handler is installed before planning; the first Ctrl-C cancels cooperatively (exit 1, cancelled files listed in `batch_done`). [K]
- A second Ctrl-C exits 3 at once. Outputs are written atomically, so an abort leaves no partial `.dds`; it can leave `.sdc-*.tmp` files and a just-renamed output without a record (`OUT-UNKNOWN-EXISTING` next run; `--on-existing overwrite` replaces it). With `--json`, an aborted run's stream ends without `batch_done`. [K]
- `convert` prints its progress on stderr; with `--json`, stdout carries only JSON Lines for every verb. [J]
- Files skipped on purpose do not make the batch exit 1: existing `.dds` inputs (`IN-DDS-SKIPPED`), outputs skipped by an explicit `--on-existing skip`, and raw packing inputs. Unresolved and blocked files, `OUT-UNKNOWN-EXISTING` and failures still exit 1; intentional skips are still reported as `file_skipped` events and counted in the summary. [I]
- `validate` infers the alpha policy (mip-0 alpha ≥ 254 everywhere is `remove`, else the sub-type's alpha-keeping default or `data`; `--alpha-policy` overrides) and sets `alpha.exact_opaque` off (a third-party BC7 decoding alpha to 254 passes; `--exact-opaque` asks for 255). A DDS in a format sdc never writes gets header checks only; a clean header is "not judged", exit 0. [J]
- The test-only hooks (`SDC_TEST_PANIC_ON`, `SDC_TEST_PANIC_ON_PLAN`, `SDC_TEST_FAIL_VALIDATION`) sit behind the engine cargo feature `test-hooks`, off by default and compiled out of `cargo build --release -p sdc`; engine and CLI dev-dependencies enable it. [K]
- `--set helper.<field>=` is refused unscoped (`helper.role is set per file: use file:<glob>.helper.role=<…>`); a `subtype:` scope is refused too. [DY1a]
- `--mem-budget 0` is a usage error; omit the flag for 50% of RAM. [K]
- A closed stdout or stderr pipe is handled, not a panic. [BC1, BQ4]
- The CLI never fills `gras_diffuses` and has no scan: tag grass in `sdc.toml`. [X11, CN59]
- The CLI runs Also export too; a failed PBR run sets the exit code as CN56 and CN58 say. [CN56, CN57, CN58]
- Removed: `.dds.json` sidecars, `patch-header`, `presets diff|export|validate`, `--fill`, `sdc encoders`, `--strict-cubemaps`. [FM22]
- The CLI accept default stays `none`. [FC1]

### GUI: Files and Inspector

- The GUI writes the same bytes as the CLI: export saves `sdc.toml` first, so `sdc convert <folder>` reproduces the last export; the GUI re-resolves unsaved edits in memory. [N4]
- The GUI's automatic decisions (High accepts, evidence-driven sub-type and alpha, a mesh `remove` included) are recorded in the project. [FC1, HR2]
- Anything the user must act on is a Problems row with its action; hand remedies are row buttons; a feature clash offers to turn off either feature. [FC3, FC10, FN5]
- No codes in the UI: rows and banners say what to do; the code is on hover and in the Messages log. A row about a missing or unused map says which. [FB8, HC2]
- One word per concept (Words section in `docs/gui.md`), one accent colour. [FC9]
- No surprising defaults or silent data effects. [FC12]
- Every edit control on the texture panel waits for its (partial) plan; navigation does not. [FK2, FK3, FL6]
- Fast re-plan: an edit plans only the edited sets and splices them into the full plan; the result equals a full plan. [FL3, FL4, FL5, FL7]
- Textures filters hide, never deselect; "Not in a set" is a clickable count. [CD13, P15]
- Textures pills, Filter ▾ and the tile pair; unselected pills are framed; Filter ▾ stays open while its combos are used. [CD10, CD21, CD22]
- Textures combos: Sub-type, Output format, Set membership (Any, In a set, Not in a set); each lists Any, the values present and a saved value the folder lacks; a texture with no job matches no value; name and choice share one box. [AG46, AG53]
- Sort: Name (default), Source size (largest first) or Status (errors, warnings, undecided, ok); ties by name. Filters, sort and tile size live in `gui.toml`; a bad value falls back to its default. [AG4, AG47, AG50]
- Tile size is a slider from 64 to 192 pt, default 96; the narrowest column stays sized for 96. Thumbnails are at most 128 px. [AG49, AV2]
- With every texture hidden the column says "No textures match the filters (N hidden)" with Clear filters (resets toggles, combos and search, not sort or tile size). [AG51]
- The selection is keyed, so it survives a sort or filter change; arrows, Shift-range, `[`, `]` and Ctrl+A all read the one displayed list, cached per change (not per frame). [AG48, AG52]
- Multi-select: a bulk edit is one step and one plan; Copy from active; Reset all. [Z1, Z2, Z3, Z5, Z6]
- Bulk sub-type runs the single-texture edit per texture (PBR follow-on applies; a texture's own write wins). Bulk leaves out recipes, presets, regex, hidden modifier keys, path/rename/output edits, Advanced drawer fields and set-level settings. [Z4, Z8]
- A bulk or copied DDS tag that does not fit a texture's format is skipped as "not valid for this map" and counted in "applies to K of N"; a bulk `null` reads "- not set". [AF5, FB4]
- Bulk edit feedback follows the document; Undo back to the saved text, or an edit that changes no text, leaves it clean. [FB3, CV18]
- Undo keeps document snapshots, 200 steps; a mode switch on a match pill is one undo step; writing the stored value is no edit. [P9, BS9, BS11]
- Header Undo / Redo follow `can_undo` / `can_redo` (hover "Undo: <label>  (Ctrl+Z)"); "Undid: <label>." leads the status text until the next plan or 5 s; Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y wait while a Sets-column box is open. [AG12, AG15, AG16]
- A config error is blamed only on the edit that started the plan; a wrongly blamed `sdc.toml` gets its edits back; the banner's Reload with unsaved edits asks Discard and reload / Cancel (Enter cancels). [BN1, BN2, FC14]
- A startup banner ("Settings were not read, defaults are used") survives re-plans until **Dismiss**. [AM17]
- Crash edits restore themselves on the next open; only a user's save removes a `.recovery` file; a second crash keeps the first. [FB5, BY3, BR10]
- A lost graphics device restarts the app and reopens the folder. [FC4, DN]
- Opening a folder closes every dialog and resets the Sets column's drop state and Slot commands. [AE2, BS5]
- A bad `sdc-project.toml` makes sets and JSON read-only, shown as a red Project row with Open in editor and Reload. [P8]
- Input names and Inputs renames are refused while `sdc-project.toml` is read-only; a rename moves each renamed set's entry in the same undo step and is blocked when two rows would write one output. [BR3, BO1, CV16]
- The rename dialog draws from a cached plan (view key plus project revision); Apply plans for itself; its preview rows use `show_rows`. [FA5, FA6]
- Sets: any set can be deleted and its files go to no set; Remove member puts a file in no set too; a set whose files are all gone still shows, as missing; delete and order are GUI-only. [P2, P3, P18, HF1]
- Moving, sharing or duplicating onto a filled slot asks Replace / Cancel. [P19]
- `add` / `remove` keys compare as the engine does (`names_file`), so an extension-less key is found; moving a file back into the set that removed it lifts that `remove`; Duplicate into set skips names another file is read as. [BR4, BR5, AG100, CV20]
- Reordering a set to its own place is no edit and leaves `sdc-project.toml` clean. [BS8]
- A slot card takes a drop only where it shows (its clipped rect). [BS4]
- Set cleanup: "Remove from set" on the missing-member chip; leaving Advanced Skin reverts the diffuse sub-type; a sub-type the user changed stays theirs. [DU]
- The Sets column lays out only the cards on screen and takes the keyboard; a changed filter scrolls it to the top (HP5). [CF7, FM32, HP5]
- Sets show order numbers; with a Sets filter on, Move to… works by real index while drag and Move up / down are off ("Clear the filter to reorder sets"). [CF4, CF5, CF6, CD15]
- Sets filter: "Search sets" (folds like set ids, tests id or label), pills Not ready / Ready / No JSON and Filter ▾ with Any and the shaders, AND-ed; every count is the keyed sets a click would show, a zero pill is weak but clickable (HP4); no "No sets match" before the first plan. [CD11, CD12, CD17, HN7, HP4]
- A selected set the filter hides shows "{id} is hidden by the Sets filter" with Clear filter; a set made by a drop under a filter stays shown until the filter changes; the line wraps with Clear filter on the row below. [CD13, FC13, HN5]
- Set cards and slot chips in the header layout; drop target outlined; helpers dock as badges on the chips they fill, card titles elide in the middle, the card's shader combo has its own right-aligned row (HP3). [CD7, CD8, CD20, HN1, HN2, HN6, HP1, HP3]
- The set editor's slots are grid rows (Slot, thumbnail, File, Format, Flags, Status): the file name truncates (name and size on hover), Flags are icon toggles "Match on this" and "Opaque", Status is a dot; scroll bars always show. [CC1, CC2, CC21]
- A selected set gets a right panel with tabs **Material** (**Set** on a vanilla set) and **Advanced** last; `advanced_open` is shared by the tab and the drawer. In 3D the Advanced drawer is drawn and the set panel is not. [CC3, CC4, CC5]
- The set header shows only the input name box (160-480 pt, grows with its text, wraps when short of room); on a JSON set "TruePBR JSON editor…" sits on the right and the truncated name takes the rest. [AO5, BH1, DO1]
- On a TruePBR JSON set a match combo ("Match 1 · diffuse" to "Match 8 · backlight"; box 64 pt, 2D only) picks the slot the ▾ list works on; a slot 2-8 pick stores `match{n}` with `\` and renames nothing; All slots lists every known texture; only the user moves it. [DO4, DO5]
- Offered input-name paths drop `textures/` when the project's own names have none; the empty list says "No known paths under {folder}…" and "No known paths yet" only for an empty scan. [DO2, DO3]
- Path-picker popups (Input name…, the JSON editor's two ▾ pickers) close on outside click, Esc or a pick; other menus and combos are unchanged. [AG125]
- Input aliases live in `sdc.toml`; output names; automatic PGPatcher rename. [AA1, AA2, AA3]
- Input name editor: a text box with grey completion and a ▾ list; no PGPatcher outputs in it; one card per PGPatcher slot. [AO1, AO2, AO4, AO7, AO8]
- Slot context menus (cards and pill thumbnails): slot commands, "From another set", Lock / Unlock. A lock keeps the fill and refuses drags; bulk edits and Reset all skip it ("slot locked"); slot locks always apply, unlike field locks. [Z9, Z10]
- Rename (Inputs) uses the whole path, no New column; a case-only rename is an alias; renames onto another file's output block. [CG1, CG2, BR1, BR2]
- Fill from names checks clashes inside its batch; a dropped file keeps the slot 3/7 clash guard. [AG99, CV13, CV14]
- Status colours are fixed; Copy works on rows. [CA20, CA21]
- The Problems list owns every fix button; `fix_off()` is the one definition of "fix buttons off". [CA11, CA12]
- One type picker for Set type and the texture panel's Sub-type; a Problems row accepts its suggestion in one click, Enter or Space. [FB9]
- Plain slot and match labels; a one-line Textures heading. [FB13]
- The set editor is one scrolling column; its room comes from Textures first, then Sets (HP6). [FB2, HP6]
- Window and layout live in `gui.toml` (not eframe persistence), debounced, in native points so another egui zoom restores the same window; a clamped window never overwrites its saved size. [AG3, AG17, AG19, AG105, CE21, FM31]
- A column's content never runs wider than its panel (egui moves the divider to the content's edge and leaves an unpainted strip). [AG124]
- Keyboard: one action list; a disabled action still owns its key; keys honour "enabled"; Ctrl+A selects the visible textures. [AG22, AG32, CV7, CD14]
- One dispatcher (`actions::keys`) runs first in the frame, most modifiers first. Arrows follow the displayed order (Up / Down a grid row as last drawn); `[` / `]` expand previous / next; 1-6 pick the channel; PageDown / PageUp step the mip; F1 or `?` opens the shortcut sheet; Ctrl+K the palette, also from a text field. [AG23, AG24, AG25, AG26]
- Save, header Export and Review's Export blur a focused text field on press so it commits and the release clicks; Ctrl+S in a text field ends the edit and saves next frame. [FC8, FC14]
- Explorer requests and file pickers: one dialog at a time, run on a thread through `model::picker`, each requester with its own owner id; stale and cancelled results are dropped. A folder pick always opens (through the unsaved-edits and export-running dialogs); ExportFolder and SaveReport results are dropped once another folder is open. [AG34, AG41, AG40, AG42, AG45, AG129]
- The export-folder button is off while an export runs; an export queued behind a plan never starts over an open dialog (it is dropped with a message). [AG129, BV10]
- Set ids, the set prefix and the input-name auto id use the export folder the current sets were planned with, so an edit before a re-plan lands is saved under the right key. [BV8]
- Toasts and the Messages panel share one store. A toast lasts 4 s (8 s warning or error), at most 3 show (queue 5; a new one replaces a waiting one of its kind), no input, no fade, hover freezes it. Decisions stay dialogs. Scan-database read errors are Messages warnings, once per text. [AG113, AG114, AG112, AG120a, BI6]
- Hover text on controls; text fields guard shortcuts. [AG14, AV-UI23]
- Per-frame cost: caches for filtered lists, stats and header reads; nothing heavy per frame. [CF8, AV-UI7, BI3, BI4]
- Each texture panel shows "Output: FORMAT · W×H · N mips · SIZE" (exact bytes on hover); the status line shows "≈ VRAM: selection X · folder Y" (Ready and NeedsDecision jobs only); sizes cached per plan revision, no file read on the UI thread. [AG118, AG119, AG120]
- No streaming thumbnails, a progress bar only: `done/total · elapsed · files/s · ETA`; skips count in done/total, not the rate. [AG94, AG35, AG39]
- A skipped DDS input's tile shows its stored image (largest mip ≤ 128 px, decoded off the UI thread). [FI1]
- An all-DDS set resolves like any set, so the GUI lists it. [FI2]
- A DDS input is re-encoded from a button (texture panel or bulk panel), which writes the `reencode` selector to its `[files]` entry as one undo step. [FI3, S6]
- Automatic recording alone never makes the document dirty: only a user edit raises the close, open or reload dialog. [FI4, FC1]
- The texture panel, set editor and bulk panel scroll separately; the centre remembers what it last showed. [FI6]
- The Preset inspector is headed with the selected texture's name and shows readable field names. [FI9]
- Open folder… starts in the parent of the most recent project. [FI10]
- A texture panel row's hint truncates with the full text on hover; its ↺ never leaves the panel. [FI12]
- A set card uses a one-row heading only when the name gets at least 80 px, or its whole width when that is less (HP12). [FI13, HP12]
- The JSON editor is called `PGPatcher JSON` everywhere. [FI14]
- The header has no Mode control. [FI16]
- The input name box has no path lock. [FI17]
- The header is one row: Open, Undo, Redo, MO2…, Preset combo, view tabs, column eyes (Textures view with a folder open), then Export to ▾, Save and Export right-aligned (twice the interact height). Below about 1280 pt the Export to / Save / Export group wraps whole to a second line. [CD1, CD2, AG126, BH6]
- The view tabs "Textures" / "Meshes" (accessible "Textures view" / "Meshes view") are equal-width, top-rounded, the selected one filled and bold. The column eyes are toggles "Textures" / "Sets" (accessible "Textures column" / "Sets column"). [BH8, BH15]
- One MO2… button holds the scan source, named on hover; its popup has the instance path with Choose…, the profiles, Scan a folder…, and Scan or Cancel. The scan row shows progress only (spinner, bar with ETA, Cancel). [AM1, AM2]
- With no folder the Preset combo's entries are greyed ("Open a folder first"); the Open menu holds Open folder… and the recent folders. [CD3]
- With no folder open the centre shows the hint, one "Open folder…" button (240 x 44), the Recent links and "Or drag a folder onto the window."; columns and the Advanced drawer are not drawn. [CD18]
- The slot menu reads `Pin this slot (always use this file)` / `Unpin this slot` and `Keep the original texture (PGPatcher …)`; keys and behaviour as AN3/AN4. [FI11, AN3, AN4]
- No bundled fonts: Windows system fonts; the CJK fallbacks `msyh.ttc`, `YuGothM.ttc` and `malgun.ttf` load from `%WINDIR%\Fonts`, each optional, after Segoe UI Emoji. [N10, BR13]
- Exe icon and version resource. [AY1, AY2]
- A path that is not Unicode does not stop `gui.toml` saving; an unreadable `gui.toml` is kept. [CV21, BR8]
- Test seams other tests rely on: `sync_layout_at(ctx, now)` with a temp `gui.toml`; `PickerBackend` fakes; `MeshesModel::procs` with `meshes::no_procs` so the host's PGPatcher cannot pause scans; `rename::plan_calls()`. [AG20, AG44, AG111, FA8]
- OPEN: defer the full plan's `request()` by one poll. [FL]

### GUI: Settings panel

- The TruePBR editors show only the active feature cards; Displacement scale sits under Material. [FM33]
- Framed groups and readable labels; header values stay inside the visible edge. [CC7, CC20]
- Group headers carry their value (Subsurface Off / Standard / Foliage; Coat / Emissive / Glint / Fuzz on/off; NiAlphaProperty set/off, closed at start; Matching "{n} match(es)"); a blocked feature reads `on, blocked by {feature}` in amber, closed and ticked. Off-state title "PGPatcher JSON settings (not written)"; titles "Coat", "Subsurface", "CS PBR records". [CC8, CC10, CC11, CC22, CC13]
- Encoding follows type. [Z11]
- The Filter combo reads `filter_allowed`: disallowed filters are greyed with the reason. [S-mips-10, FM28]
- Coat settings group under the Multilayer checkbox (the schema makes `coat_*` require it); member textures switch features on as defaults, not writes; stored coat values stay but are not written while multilayer is off. An explicit `hair` keeps multilayer, fuzz and the rest off. A coat normal turns multilayer on, so a filled coat slot greys Fuzz and the reverse. [AJ1, AJ7, AJ8, AJ2, AJ4, AJ9, AJ11]
- Every colour row draws through `ui::color::color_field`: a swatch with popup plus a `#RRGGBB` box (`#` optional, any case); invalid text turns red and does not commit; Enter or focus loss commits, Esc reverts. TruePBR colours stay float channels. The writer uses the resolved value (`json_entries`). [AG2, AG9, AG10, AJ5, AJ10]
- TruePBR panel: what `matchN` holds, number ranges and clamping; the + menu. [V1, V5, V6, V10]
- `match1` absent is the derived path (on by default), `""` is off; `match2`-`match8` are on when present. Stored values have no `.dds`; the JSON gets no leading `textures\` and `.dds` once; `match_diffuse` / `match_normal` are never written; typing slot 1 as the derived path stores no key. [V2, BS12]
- `rename` is a file name or a path (one `.dds` cut): a file name keeps the prefix from the lowest active `matchN` under `textures\pbr\`; a path is the whole prefix, relative to `Data`, written as slot commands (Note HJ5). The TruePBR box: visual settings, Matching, then collapsed Advanced TruePBR settings (`path_contains`, `nif_filter`, `rename`, UV, vertex, locks, inner UV scale, coat colour). [V3, V7]
- A set's slot cards are the picked shader's usable slots, filled or empty, plus filled slots it cannot use (amber outline, hover reason), ordered by PGPatcher slot 1-8. [V8]
- Advanced skin card is separate from TruePBR, with non-inheritable fields and UI-only ranges. [AG79, AG81, AG83]
- The skin card has the 22 CS Advanced Skin parameters in the contract's key names, in six groups: Specular, Physical, Edge & detail, Transmission, Fuzz, Wet (HB1, HB5); stored in the set's reserved `skin` table (explicit values; keys outside the 22 are dropped on the next edit, HB5), 4 decimals, one undo step per edit (a drag merges), never bulk; `_rfaos`, `_sk`, `_wet`, `_detail_n` members show as text. [AG80, AG82, AG84, AG85, AG89]
- Fuzz colour starts at .04/.08 grey, fuzz strength 1 when enabled; RGB fields allow values above 1.0. [AG133]
- Subsurface mode selector with an auto mode that can be pinned. [AG101, AG102, AG110]
- Subsurface pitfalls are warnings: both keys on; Foliage on a set whose meshes are not Tree_Anim; Standard on Tree_Anim meshes. [AG104]
- NiAlphaProperty card: keys and defaults from the schema; no sorter has no effect in Skyrim SE. [AG27, AG28, AG131]
- An alpha flag change is one `set_json_value` of the composed integer; "edit raw" takes 0-65535 (else red, not written); every set key has a reset; an out-of-range stored value is "set and invalid". Flags or thresh with `alphaprop_create` off, and `alphaprop_delete` with another alpha key, are export warnings, never corrected. + menu keys start at 4844 / 128 / true. [AG29, AG30, AG31, AG33]
- Every number key has a `DragSpec` (speed, decimals, log) in `KEYS`, used by the TruePBR card and the JSON editor; log keys use `max(speed, |value| * 0.01)`; clamps and stored values are unchanged; a rounded value shows "Stored value: {v}" on hover. [AG121, AG122, FC13]
- Glint starting values sit inside the observed band (owner ruling): `log_microfacet_density` 10, `microfacet_roughness` 1, `density_randomization` 5. [AG123]
- A multi-key write from one control (`set_json_values`) is one undo step and one plan. [AG105]
- PBR grass and PBR Landscape material types. [AS1, BM1]
- Complex grass is offered only in a grass context; its auto-detect needs a `textures` folder. [X3, X4, X10]
- Presets and the TruePBR JSON editor are centre-column tabs with Pop out. [FB7]
- The JSON editor respects slot locks; issues on entries; Rename refuses device and map-suffix names. [AF6, AG106, CV11]
- JSON editor header "N errors, M warnings"; Next / Prev error (F8 / Shift+F8) wrap, unfold and scroll; search is a case-insensitive substring of set id, label or any path ("showing K of N"); entries, text and issues are built once per `AppModel::json_key`. [AG107, AG108, AG109]
- In the JSON editor a pending match pill follows a mode change; removing a delete section moves its pending keys with the later sections. [CV10]
- Checks (Advanced → Checks): Low detail, Green direction, Parallax (accessible names end in "check"). Results are Problems rows at `Level::Check`: never amber, never in Review or `export_blocks`, never change "ready"; an export ends with one summary line (none after cancel or error). `CHK-GREEN-GUESS` is Info and never flips. [CL5, CL7, CL15, CL18, CL19, CL25]
- A texture whose green convention is unstated is DirectX; the settings panel's info line offers one **Treat as OpenGL (flip green)** button that writes `channels.green_convention = "opengl"`. [AW5, AW6]
- "Used by meshes (N)" is read per selected texture off the UI thread (stale answers dropped; a panic shows "Reading the scan database failed: …"); every texture path the Meshes view lists that is in the open folder gets "Open in Textures". [AG96, AG97, AG128]
- OPEN: PBR grass lints (flat-lit, opacity ≥ 1, hidden key), the SLSF2.30 sphere-normal warning, hiding the CS texture-set block for grass sets. [AS6]
- OPEN: landscape-height-in-diffuse-alpha check; vanilla multilayer "layer alpha all 0" check; `_rmaos` specular-alpha and complex `_m` env-vs-mesh checks. [CL14]
- OPEN: the optional "drop the slotN" offer. [AN]

### GUI: Meshes view

- The Meshes view is a flat NIF list titled "NIFs"; mod chips sit on the rows; the Any mod picker takes typed text; a This project pill. [GC1, GC5, GH3, GH4, GK3]
- The This project pill reads `AppModel::output_game_paths()` (every item's output, skipped DDS included, from its `textures/` component, ASCII-lowercase with `/`), handed to the Meshes side each frame; its revision moves only when the sorted list changes. [FI15, FI18]
- The visible list is cached; no read lands across a scan start or a reload (rows, the This project pill, Controllers lines); chips, NIF selection, the mod picker, texture-path clicks and a worklist fix's open wait for a settled store. [GC11, GC14, GC15, GQ4]
- The lock hover and path pickers read live lighting shapes only (`LIVE_LIGHTING_TEXSET`); the Meshes path filter folds ASCII only. [BT7, CV17]
- Live shape cards, framed and selectable; the Shapes column is locked while editing. [CZ1, CZ9, CZ12]
