- NIF edits are Review rows; Review is the only NIF export path; NIF edits need a chosen export folder; a blocked NIF edit never blocks the texture export. [GD2, GD5, GP10, FC5]
- NIF warnings and blockers are worklist rows. [GD1]
- The NIF edit panel has no Undo / Redo of its own; the Undo target is visible. [GH2, GD4]
- Review exports NIF edits without a texture folder; the Edit-mode 3D view has a right-click "Export this NIF". [GH8, GH9]
- With no folder open the header reads "Export" with nothing pending, else `Review k NIF edit(s)` (every pending edit, blocked included). [FI18, FI7]
- With a NIF selected the bottom panel is capped so the 3D frame keeps 160 px (content above scrolls); a taller user height returns where the cap no longer applies. [FI18, GH6]
- The edit panel's footer (`meshes-edit-export`, before the tab strip) shows a status line ("Ready", "Blocked: <reason>", idle "No edits to export."), "Writes <data path> to <export path>", the source-never-changed line and Review…; with no export folder it stays blocked with the reason. The button reads "Review…" and only opens Review, where the export runs; an edit whose source changed after its export is pending again. [CZ2, CZ3, CZ8, GD2, GP10, GQ1]
- Enter or Space on a focused Destructive button (Export NIF included) is not a click; a pointer or AccessKit click still presses it. [CZ4]
- The pending list is a collapsible "Pending edits (N)", closed until a row turns red. The Preview picker sits in an open "Clips (N · showing X)" section at the top of the Controllers tab; the tab has no clip editing. The left column is capped at 40 % of the width so the 3D view keeps its 240 px minimum with an edit open. [CZ5, CZ15, CZ23, GR1]
- Slot numbers count from 1 and carry the NIF index on hover. [GH7]
- Head parts (any HDPT) are head in the 3D preview. [BA1, BA2]
- A shape card's Texture set dropdown offers the texture sets plugins assign, matched by 3D index, preview only; ARMA skin textures are offered too. [GO1, GO2, GO3, GO4]
- An "Advanced Skin preview" card for FaceGen (4) and FaceGen RGB tint (5) shapes; picks reset with the model. [GN1, GN2, GN4]
- Heel height and Extra data have their own editor rules. [CZ17, CZ19, CZ20]
- The Plugin file references window, "Used by plugins" and stale-patch detection are gone. [GJ5, GJ6]

### GUI: Preview and 3D

- The preview size limit is 4096 on the largest side (or the GPU's lower limit); a DDS above it is refused; no 16K previews, no preview-size setting. [N1, FM25, BS2]
- The 2D preview shows the encoded mips at or below the limit, byte-identical to the export; gamma is handled as AQ says. [N1, AQ2]
- Texel readout, lens, exposure, tiling and heatmaps (per mip). [AG54, AG57, AG60, AG61, AG62]
- The readout shows stored values (0-255 is the stored float rounded; float to 4 decimals), its first line naming the space; a Normal also shows both vectors unpacked to -1..1 (z rebuilt for BC5/RG8), the encoded length and the angle between. [AG55, AG56]
- The heatmap is one guarded job keyed by result, mip, channel and full scale; a late job for an older key shows only if just its full scale differs ("updating…"). The legend runs 0 to full scale; the log slider is 0.005-0.5, default 0.1; it, the lens, tiling and exposure are not saved. [AG58, AG59, AG63]
- The preview has no Pin, Compare or Layout setting; the Value range and Plastic checks are gone. [FM26]
- The 2D and set previews restart when a job's bake, derive, cube or fill changes (`extra_inputs_key`, the Debug text of the four fields). A failed source shows its reason in the encoded pane with no Encode button. [FA1, FA2, DT18]
- Preview toolbar: three clusters (Channels, View, Encode); Encode now is the one filled button, shown only when it can encode. An expanded texture keeps a 520 px centre floor (480 px picture); the Advanced drawer has a 260 px minimum and keeps the user's width. [CE12, CE14, CE15, CE17]
- HDR (BC6H, RGBA16F, RGBA32F) tiles are tone-mapped `x / (1 + x)` at 0 EV, as the main preview is. [FM31]
- A cube item's preview draws the cross: Heatmap and Tile 3x3 are disabled ("Not for cubes", setting untouched), no texel readout, mip label "mip k (WxH per face)", "Face labels" on by default. A six-file cube's source pane has six face buttons (processed face); a single-image cube shows its input. [DT17, DT20, DT21, DT23]
- The cube settings section is table-driven (`CUBE_FIELDS`, 16 `cubemap.*` rows), drawn only when every selected item is a Cubemap (no bulk cube rows). Drags are bounded to the engine's range; an existing out-of-range value shows unclamped and is written only on change. Unticking or Auto / None removes the key. The layout combo never offers "Six face files". [DT7, DT8, DT9, DT10, DT11, DT13]
- The marker dialog: a kept (not ours) or failed write leaves it open with the note inside (amber / red); a successful write closes it; closing it cancels a Save still open. [DT29, DT33, DT34]
- The 3D preview: pan, reset view, sun rotation, Copy image; the set's 2D | 3D switch works in place. [AG72, AG73, AG74, AG77, AG78]
- In 3D the header's "TruePBR settings…" toggle opens one pop-out (fixed id; "<name> settings" for a member) with the member's settings, the set's TruePBR panel and the skin card, only while the centre shows 3D; its place is saved as `[layout] settings_window`. In 3D, selecting a texture of a set opens or keeps that set's view; a texture of no set shows its normal settings. [AG132, AG87, AG88]
- HDRI yaw and RGB background; debug views; a missing texture is filled. [AU3, AU4, AU7]
- Render parity with CS's deferred path: "Accurate (deferred)" is on by default with a fallback notice. [CJ18, CJ19]
- The MATO layer in the set preview is renderer-wide and session-only, from the in-game capture constants. [GG1, GG2, GG14]
- Animation clock, key editor, controllers and clips; In game (simulated) says what it simulates. [CM6, CM10, CM11, CM16, GD7]
- The 3D View menu keeps its open state; one light slider per row. [GK1, GK2]
- No Timings panel, preview timing log, frame-time footer or UV checker view. [FM25, GJ3, GJ8]

### GUI: Dialogs and Export

- One dialog frame; the Enter default is filled, a destructive button is outlined; Esc only closes things, a popup first. [CE1, CE2, CE9, FB11]
- `dialogs::modal_keys` gives `cancel` (Esc, or a click outside where allowed) and `enter` (no widget focused, not on the opening frame). A click outside does nothing in the rename dialogs, Copy settings and Fill from names. Dialogs are 420 pt wide (Slot commands 640, rename 860). [AG11, CE7, CE8]
- Button sets (! destructive, * Enter): unsaved Close / Open = Cancel, Discard!, Save; Reload with unsaved edits = Discard and reload!, Cancel*; export running = Stop the export…!, Keep exporting*; changed on disk = Reload from disk, Overwrite with my changes!, Cancel* (keeps edits); PGPatcher JSON edited = Overwrite {name}!, Keep the edited {name}*; set-drop Replace = Replace {name}!, Cancel, no default; Fill from names = Cancel, Fill*; Copy settings and Set rename are plain. [CE3, CE4, CE5, CE6]
- Enter never saves, deletes or exports; Delete set is one undo step. [FC5]
- Review is a tab, not a dialog; it never exports by itself and waits for every overwrite answer; warnings never open it. [CA1, CA2, CA5, FC5]
- Review has no Enter rule and closes only with **Close** (or by opening another folder). A failed plan leaves it open with the blockers visible; a queued Review export is re-gated on the new plan. [CA19, CA15]
- Review shows what will be written; CS PBR and complex-material JSON show their status only. [CA6, CA8]
- Review keeps its gate; the half-size question, the warning ticks and the stale-outputs list are out. [FM24]
- Review opens at 40 % of the window height (`bottom-review`); under 1200 pt its sections stack in one scroll area. Headings carry counts (`Blockers (n)`, `Undecided (n)`; a missing export folder is a blocker). The footer's NIF and JSON lists share one scroll area of at most a third of the tab (`show_rows`). [FB10, FB4, FC14]
- An edited PGPatcher JSON is a reason to open Review: **Keep the edited file** / **Overwrite it**, neither preselected; the answer stores the on-disk hash and applies only if the file still has it, else Review asks again. A cancel-and-leave racing a finished export answers "keep". An error never replaces the overwrite question. A refused CS EDID is not an orphan. [CA7, BR7, BR11, BR12]
- Low detail is not a Review question. [FC2]
- A queued Review export is re-gated and can be dropped; a reload clears the pre-answer. [CA13, CA17]
- No inherited export folder: the export folder is per project, and the header Export is neutral until the project is clean. [BV6, FB1]
- Export to ▾ holds the Also ticks, "PGPatcher JSON editor…" (cs mode, folder open, `sdc-project.toml` loaded; else hidden) and Make marker cube…; clicks inside never close it. While an export runs, Export's lower half is a progress bar (done/total, running file on hover); the drawer keeps the only Cancel. [CD4, BH7, BH9, CN74, CN77, DT27]
- The bottom panel is full width under the header in both views; collapsed, a strip of tab buttons with counts; it snaps open and shut (owner). Ctrl+M opens Messages or collapses the panel if Messages shows. The export drawer is its Files tab; no Warnings tab (per-file warnings are Files-row hovers and Problems rows). [CA9, CA10, CD5, CF17, CA16]
- The set editor's warnings block is one line "N problems in this set - Show in Problems"; a Textures-column "{n} undecided" count and a set's warning badge open Problems narrowed. [CA18]
- With no export folder chosen, nothing shows an `sdc-out` path. [FI5]
- The header's `Review …` counts every pending NIF edit; an `Export …` text counts only what it writes. [FI7]
- Enter in the command palette runs a result only after the user typed a query or moved the highlight. [FI8]
- The rename dialog ignores Enter on the frame it opens. [BY2]
- A case-only rename removes the old-case twin of a CS PBR or complex-material JSON only when a hash record exists and the twin still hashes to it. [BY10, BO4]
- The Also gate names the project as the engine does (`engine::also::project_name`). Encode now during an export waits for it and runs when it ends. [CV12, CV22]
- Cancel an open while opening; Recent. [AG92, AG93]
- Plan progress is a planner counter; scan row bar and ETA. [AG91, AG95]

### NIF crates: modscan

- Scan schema 10 (GO4/GO5); every schema bump empties scan.db until the next scan. Schema 9 dropped the dead tables, columns and writers; Plugin overrides lists every record. [GJ7, GO5]
- A tainted scan unloads plugin rows and empties the winner tables; only a completed scan refreshes the Textures view's mesh evidence. [BW, BT3, BT6]
- A plan while the scan store is unsettled keeps the mesh evidence; a scan start re-issues the mesh-evidence read in flight. [BY6, CW1, CW2]
- Failed origins keep their cache; the ASCII filter and `winners` as BE rules. [BE3, BE4, BE5]
- `Store::nifs_using_canonical_texture(slot, path, limit)` returns the same `MatchRow`s as `nifs_using_texture` for every raw spelling of one canonical texture path, through the `path.p = ?` index (a test pins the plan). It is a method, not a new `TexKey` variant, because the GUI matches `TexKey` exhaustively. [CK, CK1, CK2]
- `Store::winners()` and `Store::losers()` are two full reads, run on a worker thread from a read-only store; they replaced `winning_nifs`. [GC2]
- Lock hover and the path pickers read lighting shapes only (`shader_kind = 1`) in a non-derived copy of an enabled origin; TruePBR shapes count. [BT7]
- MO2 text files are read per line: a UTF-8 BOM is dropped, a valid UTF-8 line stays, any other line decodes as Latin-1. [DA1, CV25]
- One shared copy per string-table entry; shader properties decoded once; block type names shared per type; `Lighting.name`, `Effect.name` and `ExtraValue::Str` are `Arc<str>` and serialise as plain strings; `AvHead` decodes no name. [GA1, GB1, GB2, GB3, GB4, GB5]
- One shape-row builder; one scan read for mesh votes, alpha and Tree_Anim. [GP7, GP11]
- MATO lookup cuts the EDID the way the scan does. [GP2]
- Typed mesh-evidence queries are parked (no consumer); `sdc.exe` gets no modscan dependency; the raw-SQL wrappers in the GUI stay. [GD3, CN59]
- OPEN (NIF team): write-only modscan columns; loose-walk I/O errors (BE5's gap); scan store writes names once per row; PGPatcher `modrules.json` priority and JSON in BSAs are not read. [BE6, BQ14, BU, GA, CM]
- OPEN (NIF team): non-UTF-8 lines decode as Latin-1, so an ANSI en dash or curly quote (0x80-0x9F) in a mod or plugin name becomes a control character; the `shortcut:` in `modscan/src/lib.rs` and `qtini.rs` asks for a Windows-1252 table. [GJ9]

### NIF crates: vfs

- The loose walk follows links, reports I/O errors and skips only the actor facegen folder. [BP1, BU, CV23]
- One BSA handle; BSA folder names and modlist names as AV-S rules. [AV-S3, AV-S4, AV-S5]

### NIF crates: esp

- ARMA MODL is not a model; ARMA skin textures (NAM0 / NAM1) are read. [GO4, GO5]
- `esp` is a path dependency of sdc-gui (MATO layer). [GG16]
- `esp::mato_data` returns None for the whole plugin on any fault its walk reaches (bad group size, bad zlib, a subrecord past its record) and never clamps; a record's last EDID and last DATA count, as in the scan, and the first record whose DATA has at least 48 bytes wins. [GG11, GQ8]

### NIF crates: nif

- The writer re-opens per op, writes atomically and never writes what its parser rejects. The reader seeks by the block size array; `Header::check_file_length` and each decoder's exact-size check refuse a file whose sizes are wrong; a shape head is read inside its declared block (`BlockSize` otherwise), and an implausible block count or a bad type index has its own error. [CI1, CI4, CI5, CY2, GQ9]
- `SetBits` covers single bits and sub-fields; a shared shader refuses texture path edits; a shared alpha splits first; refused ops are conflicts in the document. [CI6, CI16, CI17, CI20]
- A shared interpolator is a conflict ("This interpolator is shared by N controllers: editing it would change all of them."), greyed in the Controllers tab. A change to a controller on a shared alpha needs "Split alpha" first; a clip-driven alpha splits too. [CI18, CI21, CR9]
- Edit paths stay inside the edit mod; NIF export follows the chosen export folder. [CI27, GD5]
- Export drops orphan blocks our edits made; doubt means no compaction. Every source block stays at its index; an appended block stays while a kept block references it (fixed point); refs are renumbered; with nothing to drop the bytes come back unchanged, so compaction is idempotent; cost is linear in blocks and types. [CQ1, CQ2, CQ3, CQ4, CQ5, CQ6]
- Only NEW validation errors block "Export NIF"; an error already in the source (a mis-named BSXFlags) shows as a warning. [CI14]
- Shader flags that need a shader type: Skin Tint and FaceGen detail are tied to their types; orphan shaders are skipped; multi-layer parallax on every type, hair soft lighting, glow map on eyes. [CS1, CS2, CS3, DK]
- Game-crash guard: SLSF2 bit 24 (Multi_Layer_Parallax) without PBR on any drawn shader other than type 11 is an `ALWAYS_BLOCK` Error (`MLP_TYPE`) with a clear-bit fix; with PBR the bit is the coat and keeps the Warn. A type change away from 11 clears bit 24 in the same undo step with a notice, unless PBR is on. Controllers are not guarded. [DJ, DJ1, DJ2, DJ3, DJ4, DK1]
- SLSF1 bit 18 Hair_Soft_Lighting off type 6 draws nothing: `ALWAYS_BLOCK` Error `HAIR_SOFT_TYPE` with a clear-bit fix, cleared on leaving type 6, checkbox greyed (`type_flag_lock`). `GLOW_ON_EYE` and `EYE_ENV_TYPE` are non-blocking Warns with a clear-bit fix. No "type needs flag" rules; parallax off type 3 and glow on types 0 and 1 are unguarded. [DK2, DK3, DK4]
- On a PBR shape the Shader tab shows "PBR: <meaning>" beside SLSF2 bits 12, 24, 25, 26, 27, 30 (`render3d::pbr_bit_meaning`). Three warnings only: type 11 without PBR and without bit 24; bit 24 on a non-11 type; Fit_Slope (bit 12) without PBR. Soft/Rim/Back lighting and Effect_Lighting are never warned. [CI26]
- Emissive Multiple's soft range is 0-100; outside it only warns. [CM35]
- Key data: `nif::keys` reads and encodes NiFloatData, NiPosData and NiBoolData; any other class is an error; `key_type` stays a raw `Option<u32>` so an unknown type round-trips. [CM1]
- Evaluator follows NifSkope's `ctrlTime`; CONST is a step; QUADRATIC is Hermite with unscaled tangents. Play Backwards phase is unverified (`shortcut:`). Two controllers on one variable: the last in the chain wins. [CM3, CM5, CM24, CM30]
- Blend interpolators are read by `ctrl::read_blend_interp`; `read_interp` still reports them `known: false`. [CM2]
- A managed controller's Variable can change; each bound block's Controller ID is rewritten only when it equals the old variable's decimal text. [CM13]
- Clip key edits use one binding rule with copy-on-write by user count; a clip with several interpolators for one controller refuses ("...they cannot be edited."). [CM14, CR13]
- Add key appends at last time plus the average key spacing (0.1 for one key), strictly after the last key (next f32 when the step rounds away; off when none exists). Editing a free controller's keys sets its stop to the last key's time and its start to the first key's only when earlier. `validate_ops` checks only the last `SetKeys` per (controller block, clip). [CM33, CM34]
- AddController times must be finite: "Controller times must be finite numbers" blocks export. [CM32]
- Controllers tab: curve graph, key table, Linear/Quadratic combo, no Play controls; the key cache is rebuilt in `reapply`, never per frame. PBR shapes relabel LSCF 9 "Specular level", LSCF 10 "Roughness scale", LSCC 0 by coat/subsurface bit. A new `Op` variant updates every exhaustive match in the same commit, including gui `keep_base`. [CM12, CM15, CM29]
- Clock: a new clock is paused at the start, looping, 1x; Speed 0.1x-4x; one frame advances at most 1/30 s. The preview animates LSCF 20-23, 11, 9, 10, 12, LSCC 0 and 1 and TestRef; LSCF 8 changes only an Environment Map shader. [CM4, CM8]
- Amber no-effect warnings (row and worklist, never blocking): Specular off or strength 0 when the mesh loads (LSCF 9, 10, LSCC 0), alpha test off, refraction strength, two controllers on one variable. Specular is fixed at load, so those controllers do nothing on a shape loaded without it. [CM22, CM26]
- Env map scale (LSCF 8) on a PBR shape crashes the game: an export-blocking Error; on a shape PGPatcher makes PBR it is a red In game line plus an amber Warning (cached simulation), not blocking. An empty RMAOS slot is an amber row (CS's default RMAOS is fully metallic). [CM25, GD6, CM27, CM32]
- `nif::clips` encoding: a Float or Colour block's Controller ID is the variable in decimal; a TestRef's Controller ID and every Interpolator ID are null; Node Name is the shape's own name index. A nameless or duplicate-named shape is refused by a split that adds CBs. The object palette lists each animated shape once, entries never removed; a shape rename patches CB Node Names and the palette together. [CR2, CR3, CR4]
- `read_model` lists only live sequences (those a manager lists, or all when there is no manager). [CR6, CR7]
- Clips UI: the Controllers tab offers no clip editing; the picked clip stays picked only while a sequence at that block keeps its name, else the preview returns to Free chains. [CU1, GR1]
- Preview of clips: a clip named SpecialIdle (ASCII case-insensitive) plays with its own frequency, range and cycle and wins; every other managed controller holds the first key of the first listed clip. Two managers or two SpecialIdle clips on one controller: both play, the track applied last wins. [CR11, CR12, CU5]
- Pinned, no code: the var-8 check runs outside the simulation cache. [CU6, CU7]
- Controller edits never move to a twin; Add controller refuses a chain whose tail another property lists. [CY1, CY4]
- Validate decodes texture paths and BSX names as Windows-1252. [GA3]
- One node test: a root of any NiNode subclass gets its object net. [GP3]
- `nif::text::label` caps a name inside any per-shape message at 256 chars plus "..."; use it for every message that names a shape. [GA7]
- Edit session (Meshes view): while a NIF edit is open, header Undo / Redo, palette and Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y act on the NIF history via `actions::keys`; a slider drag is one pending edit; the preview rebinds a known texture path without reload; "Show only this shape" hides the others; unmapped blend pairs draw as stored, badged "Not verified in game"; after export "In game" asks to re-run PGPatcher; leaving the edit preview says so. [CI10, CI11, CI12, CI13, CI19, CI22, CI23, CI28]
- Edit source and documents: `pick_source` compares roots ignoring path spelling and never takes the edit mod's own copy or any `sdc NIF Edits` folder, nor any folder holding our edit document for this NIF. An autosave is deleted only when this session holds it; a session stays unsaved until its save succeeds; a root change waits for the live edit to move (else blocked with the reason, retried when that file changes); the newer of autosave and folder document wins. [CV1, GD11, GD12, GD13, GD20, GD21, GD24, GD27, GD28, GQ3]
- Never overwrite edits the session did not show: an autosave or folder edit document this session never loaded is moved aside to `<stem>.<unix seconds>.toml` before the save, the name reserved with `create_new`. [GD19, GD25, GD26]
- Review answers (Keep / Overwrite) are dropped when the edit ends, a folder change is taken or a run is cancelled; a cancel during the Also walk writes no NIF edits. Review's NIF row has `Diff… <data path>`; the hand-overs run each frame in `app.rs` in a fixed order (clear answers, answer, due export, diff request, rows and problems). [GD29, GF1, GF2, GF3, GF4, GF8, GF9]
- Worklist rows merge only repeats of one target; every field kind takes focus; the edit's blocking reason is a row only when no other row says it; a malformed edit document's error names its file. The NIF edit footer is idle before any edit, with or without a folder. [GD14, GD15, GD30, GD33, GH5]
- Source side shows PGPatcher-supplied paths as one grey line, not "Missing texture"; one In game / Source label pair. [GH1]
- Meshes list: picking a mod never deselects; a reload keeps the picked mod while it has NIFs and is not derived, else Any mod, and reselects the NIF it holds (picked under Any mod, or under a picked mod while the store is not settled) once the store settles. Wins hides under Any mod; Overridden lists every losing copy; Conflicted is `n_copies > 1`; Problems is a problem or an LE mesh. Search matches path (no `meshes/`) or mod, ASCII case-insensitive, `\` as `/`; the texture chip (`users_of_texture`) combines with it; "Show in Meshes" sets Any mod, clears search and chips. Row chips list warnings first; "In game" is short, full text as its AccessKit name. [BT5, GC4, GC6, GC7, GC8, GC9, GC10, GC16, GC17, GC19, GC20, GQ4]
- OPEN (NIF team): two managers on one controller untested beyond the pinned case. [CR14]

### NIF crates: render3d

- Built-in preview draws depth-test LESS_EQUAL. [CI24]
- Replay draws the captured geometry; MainRW is RT0; golden thresholds as CJ8; env cube as CJ15. [CJ4, CJ5, CJ8, CJ15]
- Golden fixtures come from the golden-capture workflow (`SDC_GOLDEN`, folder `cs-1.9.0-d2026.10.11.0`), keyed by CS version; scene WhiterunDragonsReach01 (hour 7.5, Linear Lighting on); skin and hair specular follow CS's default settings, as bundled (GT4). [CJ1, CJ2, CJ3]
- `golden-baseline.txt` lists every subject's outcome; any change, or a missing, empty or subject-less baseline, fails. Replay seeds the captured prepass depth and draws with the captured depth state (Equal), failing closed. A subject runs from its real mask population (an overlay or empty mask is skipped with the reason; an empty clean sub-mask is "no-clean-composite", MRTs still asserted); 8 outputs add DEFERRED. [CJ9, CJ10, CJ11]
- Deferred frames run the bundled `DeferredCompositeCS.hlsl` (`CSHADER`, GT4) into RT0; blended shapes draw forward on top; debug views skip it; a failed build keeps forward with "Deferred preview unavailable: <error>". A late composite failure rebuilds forward programs synchronously; async deferred-off keeps the target while any draw holds DEFERRED; no DEFERRED draw means a forward frame. [CJ6, CJ7]
- `extraction_matches_capture` prints, never asserts; `trace_values_match_unit_functions` counts a trace only where replay depth is within 2 D24 steps of SV_POSITION.z. The perf gate uses fresh frames; the GUI cannot see which permutation was drawn. [CJ13, CJ14, CJ20]
- Open limits: an opaque forward shape winning over a DEFERRED pixel keeps its RT4/RT5 (`shortcut:` in `render`); Dynamic Cubemaps rebuild the env cube on the CPU per yaw change (11-16 ms); NorRubble's STAT alternate texture set is not applied by `Renderer::load`. [CJ22, CJ23]
- A composite test accepts round-to-nearest-even or toward-zero on f16 channels (D3D allows both); env cube sizes are checked in u64, capped at 16384, non-power-of-two is an error. [CJ16, DL4]
- Per-frame animation work is cbuffer-only. [CM7]
- The panorama maps `u = 0.5 - (atan2(y, x) + YAW) / 2pi` (clockwise like a 360 photo) with `YAW = 0.774`; one definition serves `dir_to_uv`, `uv_to_dir`, `cube_faces` and the backdrop. [DL2, DL3]
- The env cube's mips are CS's specular irradiance, built by CS's SpecularIrradianceCS on the GPU; the CPU chain samples the texel corner. [GL1, GM1, GM2]
- Advanced Skin without RFAOS needs no default (profile values apply). Wetness drives CS's skin wetness code (`skinPerGeometry.x`, t74 sets `skinMaterialFlags.y`), compiling `WETNESS_EFFECTS` only for shapes with wetness on; `MaterialState::skin_preview` makes a pick `Textures`, wetness `Permutation`, a slider `Cbuffer`. [GL2, GN3, GN5]
- One compare-func mapping in replay; missing cbuffer fields are reported after async compiles; the shader cache path comes from `main`. [GP1, GP5, GP6]
- Device errors keep the `DEVICE_REMOVED` prefix with the context appended after (`device::gpu_error`), because the GUI's replay check is `starts_with`. [GA4, CX2]
- `settle` compiles the keys the pool already took; `set_material` evicts every texture-less missing entry and keeps decode-error entries; a pick (`report_missing`) reloads a path cached as an absent Auto guess; a replaced model load drops its queued shader compiles. [BT1, BT2, CX1, CV24, GQ7]
- `diffuse_curve` without a white-mid gap gives the default curve; `load_fixture` refuses a zero crop. [GA5, GA6]
- `copies_of_path` reads the copy's own derived flag, so a mesh PGPatcher wrote into overwrite is not a source. [CX3]
- The golden/replay comparison harness is `test-hooks` only. [GJ4]
- The bundled studio HDRI is Poly Haven's 1k file. [GJ1]
- A PBR shape with the vanilla default glossiness 80 renders black (existing). [GG17]
- MATO snow/ash layer (Set preview toggle): the record is the first scan `edids` match (ASCII case-insensitive), read on a worker (one plugin at a time, keyed cache); a missing or bad record draws default coverage (falloff 0.35 / 0.4, noise 48) with a grey line, never an error; so does a missing projected texture (`Renderer::projected_missing()`). Skinned shapes, shader types 3, 4, 5, 11, 14, 16, vanilla SLSF2.24 and SLSF1.12 skip the layer (a PBR SLSF2.24 coat keeps it, GZ1). Bad JSON values take CS defaults (one bad `baseColorScale` component resets all three; glint only for boolean `true`). Changed layer textures show after a toggle off and on. Glint noise ports `noisegen.cs.hlsl`; vertex colour follows `has_vertex_colors`. [GG3, GG4, GG5, GG6, GG7, GG8, GG9, GG10, GG12, GG13, GG19, GG20, GG21]
- OPEN (NIF team): replay of blended subjects. [GG]

### NIF crates: pgpsim

- pgpsim simulates PGPatcher 2.3.1: SLSF1.21 shapes are patched; `pbr` and `env_*` are ignored; wrong-typed patch keys are ignored key by key; a crashing JSON stops the whole run; complex material honours `disabled`. [GE1, GE3, GE4, GE9, GE11]
- A wrong-typed match key is `SimResult::Error` with the text `PGPatcher 2.3 crashes on this JSON: <key> is not a <type>` (`pgpsim::CRASHES`); the crash lines come before In game's eligibility exits; a crashing run never reverts, so a PG_STOCK shape gets only the crash lines. The Textures set preview shows the same crash through `MeshesModel::pg_crashes`. [GE5, GE14, GE15, GE16]
- `shape_matches` ignores `nif_filter` for FaceGeom and BodySlide ShapeData paths; the set preview leaves `nif_filter` to it. A non-string `nif_filter` there still errors. [GE2, GE10, GE12]
- A shape with `NiStringExtraData` `PG_STOCK` gets the amber first line "In game: PGPatcher first reverts this shape to the stock state recorded in its PG_STOCK block; the preview shows it as loaded." and the revert is not simulated. [GE6]
- `pgpsim::SIMULATED_AS` reads "Simulated as PGPatcher 2.3.1: loose JSON only, no modrules priority, read at the last scan". In game scope: loose `PBRNIFPatcher\**\*.json` of Data, enabled mods and overwrite; one file wins per shape by MO2 priority then path; a Skip file lets the next win. Not covered: JSON in BSAs, TXST alternate textures, plugin-use and blocklist eligibility, CM and parallax competition; only lighting shapes with a texture set; a folder scan reads no JSON. JSON is read on a worker, kept across switches. [GE7, GE8, CM17, CM18]
- There is no `_m.json` importer; Tessalith never overwrites an `_m.json` it did not write. [GE13]
- `alphaprop_*` keys; `MatchIndex`. [AU2, CM19]
- OPEN (NIF team): GE's final-review follow-ups M4-M7 (cache `pg_crashes` lines, etc.). [GE17]

### Process and docs

- Every judgement call is a ruling; rulings live in `.claude/DECISIONS.md`, history in `.claude/DECISIONS-1.x.md`, superseded 2.x rulings in `.claude/DECISIONS-ARCHIVE.md`. [FH1, FH3]
- Documentation is current state only, one home per fact, with hidden `<!-- D:ID -->` ruling markers checked by `tools\doc-markers.ps1`. [FH1, FH5]
- Agent-facing files live in `.claude/`; mistakes go to `.claude/mistakes/<section>.md`, temporary files to `.claude/scratch/`. [FH6]
- A DECISIONS.md conflict after a rebase: take the other side's DECISIONS.md and DECISIONS-ARCHIVE.md, note `$base = git rev-parse HEAD`, re-run `tools/decisions-archive.py --apply` with every table in `tools/decisions-archive/`, then `--check --base $base`. `--apply` is idempotent. [FH2, FH4]
- An encode-version arm moves only when that type's output changes; there is no test-only override table in shipped code, and only a type whose arm moved re-encodes after an upgrade. [DM6, DM7]
- `docs/research/` is organised by format. [FM40]
- Host plugins (Photoshop, GIMP, Paint.NET, chaiNNer, `psdds`) live on branch `host-plugins`, bug fixes only; their docs and wiki pages live on that branch too (FM); `psdds` builds against the workspace `engine` by path. [FG1, FG2, FG3, FG4, FG5, FG6]
- Tests never reach `%LOCALAPPDATA%` through `SdcApp::new`. [BX]
- A kittest `run()` never settles under a live worker poll (egui subtracts kittest's 0.25 s `predicted_dt` from every repaint delay), so a tooltip test steps up to 12 frames and asserts the text. OPEN: other `hover()`-then-`run()` tests may flake the same way. [FD1, FD2, FD3]
- OPEN: code signing / AV: the release is unsigned and SmartScreen warns. [Answers 16]

## Note FM — Tessalith 2.0 (main team, 2026-11-02)

- **FM1 Strict byte-identity pins (`crates/engine/tests/encode_versions/identity.json`).** Every output the `encode_versions` fixtures write (cs, vanilla, and a coverage set: opaque BC1, BC3 and BC7 with a cutout alpha and box mips and with a blend alpha, BC4, BC5) is pinned by sub-type, format, size and the SHA-256 of the DDS pixel data (the bytes after the header). Unlike `encode_inputs.json` (the encoder's input, bumped per type by Note DM), these pins fail on any byte change. The input hash is not pinned, because a new `Profile` field changes it with no byte change. BC7 and BC6H bytes depend on the CPU's ISA path, so their hash is keyed `payload_sha256@<isa>` with `dispatched_isa()`: strict on the machine that recorded it, while another CPU adds its own field and keeps the others. This also covers the post-encode BC7 fixes (`bc7_opaque_fix`, `guard_grass_corner`) that `level_identity.rs` does not see. Re-pin rule: delete the key from `identity.json`, re-run, and name the key and the reason in an FM item. Only BC1/BC3 Dual, tent-picked alpha mips, power-of-two/resize changes and new keys may appear. Each later 2.0 task commits whatever `encode_versions` rewrites.
- **FM2 Dev and test builds keep line tables only (`Cargo.toml` `[profile.dev] debug = "line-tables-only"`, audit §4.1, 2026-11-02).** The default `debug = 2` left each integration test with a 300-480 MB PDB (the shared target held 235 GB of them). Measured on `cargo test -p sdc-gui --test nif_edit --no-run`, own target dir: PDB 462,082,048 bytes before, 148,082,688 after (-68%); exe 55,108,096 vs 55,093,248 bytes; build times were 6 min 47 s (first build, deps included) and 4 min 51 s (every crate rebuilt), not comparable. Panics and backtraces keep file:line; only local-variable inspection in a debugger is lost, and `CARGO_PROFILE_DEV_DEBUG=2` brings it back for one shell. Release builds and output bytes are untouched.
- **FM3 `eframe` loses its `links` feature (`crates/gui/Cargo.toml`, audit §4.2, 2026-11-02).** The GUI never opens a URL through egui (no `hyperlink`, `open_url` or `OpenUrl`; its `ui.link` calls are egui core's Link widget, and the Explorer actions spawn `explorer.exe`, AG34), and `links` only enabled `webbrowser` in egui-winit for one OpenUrl handler. Dropping it removes `webbrowser` and the crates only it pulled in (27 packages from `Cargo.lock`, 489 to 462 entries); `cargo tree -p sdc-gui -i webbrowser` now finds nothing and `cargo build -p sdc-gui --release --offline` is green. A future `ui.hyperlink` would silently do nothing, so the Cargo.toml comment says it; re-add the feature if one is ever wanted.
- **FM4 `engine/build.rs` watches this worktree's reflog, not the shared `refs/heads` (audit §4.3, 2026-11-02).** `watch_git` named `HEAD`, the common `refs/heads` directory and `packed-refs`, so a commit or new branch in any of the 14 worktrees re-ran the script and rebuilt engine, render3d, gui, cli and bench in all the others. It now names `HEAD`, `packed-refs` and `<git_dir>/logs/HEAD` (per worktree; git appends to it on every commit, reset and checkout made there); the `p.exists()` guard stays. Checked by hand: creating a branch (a `refs/heads` change from elsewhere) leaves `cargo build -p engine -v` showing `Fresh engine`; a commit in this worktree re-runs the script. Known limit, marked `shortcut:` in the code: with `core.logAllRefUpdates=false`, or a branch moved from outside this worktree, the `SDC_GIT_COMMIT` string can go stale until HEAD changes. Output bytes are untouched.
- **FM5 IN-UNREADABLE entries read `<path>: <reason>` for walkdir path errors (`discover.rs`, audit §4.4, 2026-11-02).** The Problems row's Open folder fix (`bottom.rs` `unreadable_folder`) takes the text before the first `": "` as the unreadable path and opens its parent. For a dangling junction or symlink and for a link loop, `discover` pushed walkdir's own text, "IO error for operation on <path>: <reason>", so the button opened a folder that does not exist (a loop gave an empty path). `discover` now writes `<path>: <io error>` for a path error and `<path>: file system loop to <ancestor>` for a loop; the depth-0 error (still fatal) and the path-less folder branch (`<unreadable child>: <err>`, an access-denied folder) are unchanged. Pinned by `a_dangling_link_is_skipped_as_its_path_then_the_reason` (a junction on Windows, a symlink on unix; it failed first with walkdir's text) and by the new shapes in `unreadable_folder_reads_the_engine_message`. The message is a skipped-entry note only; no output byte changes. A link to an ancestor folder is pinned by `a_link_loop_is_skipped_as_its_path_then_the_loop` (`<link>: file system loop to <ancestor>`); the path-error arm with no io error or loop keeps walkdir's own text (`err.to_string()`), which already names the path.
- **FM6 `load_image` no longer keeps a second full-size f32 copy (`load.rs`, `bake/mod.rs`, audit §4.5, 2026-11-02).** `decode` built the texel `Vec<[f32; 4]>` with `img.to_rgba32f().pixels().map(..).collect()`, so the decoded image, a 16 B/texel `to_rgba32f` buffer and the collected Vec were alive together. It now reads the size, then `img.into_rgba32f().into_raw()` is cast to `Vec<[f32; 4]>` with `bytemuck::allocation::try_cast_vec` (same alignment, no copy; `bytemuck` with `extern_crate_alloc` is a direct engine dependency, already in the lock through `image`; the copy fallback runs only if the allocation's capacity is not a whole number of texels). The decoded image is dropped by `into_rgba32f`, so the peak is `decoded + 16` per texel, 16 B/texel less (about 4 GiB at 16K, 256 MiB at 4K). `BakeJob::peak_bytes` is `w * h * (decoded + 16)` and its asserts follow (`bake/mod.rs` `estimated_bytes` tests: 3 + 16 and 8 + 16 per texel, the layered-normal admission 19 per layer texel; `api.rs` 16K-normal admission). Lower admission means more bake jobs can run at once under `--mem-budget`; the general `input_bytes` estimate is unchanged. Output bytes: `load_image_output_bits_are_pinned` (an 8-bit RGBA PNG, a 16-bit RGB PNG and an EXR with alpha, hashed over their `f32` bits) passed on the old code first and on the new; `golden` and `encode_versions` pass with no JSON diff.
- **FM7 `build_views` folds the saved set order into a map once (`crates/gui/src/model/sets.rs` `sort_by_saved_order`, audit §1 item 2, 2026-11-02).** After one drag in the Sets column the sort called `order.iter().position(same_id)` (two `fold_id` allocations per entry) inside every comparison, about 190M `same_id` calls at 4,000 sets on each sets-document edit. The order is now folded into a `HashMap<fold_id, first index>` and the views sort with `sort_by_cached_key` on `(index or usize::MAX, id)`; the rule is unchanged (saved order first, ids compared folded, the first listing of an id wins, the rest by raw id, equal keys stay in their input order). `order_tests::the_saved_order_sort_equals_the_linear_one` keeps the old linear sort as a reference and compares both on 20 shuffled fixtures with Unicode-folded, back-slashed and slash-wrapped ids, an id listed twice, unlisted ids and views whose ids differ only by case. GUI model only; no output byte changes.
- **FM8 The bench crate leaves `main`; it and the raw tuning data live on local branch `bench` (plan task 2, owner, 2026-11-02).** `crates/bench` (`sdc-bench`, measurement only, never shipped) and `docs/research/bc7e-tuning/*.csv` and `*.tsv` are deleted from `main`; branch `bench` keeps them, never pushed or merged, and the research pages that cite them point there. The BC4 proof moved with the code it checks: `crates/encoders-sys/tests/bc4ref.rs` pins the Rust reference (`crates/encoders-sys/tests/support/bc4ref.rs`: `upstream`, `search`) to the vendored C++ for the BC3, BC4 and BC5 entry points, and `engine_bc4_bytes_equal_reference` in `crates/engine/tests/encode.rs` pins the engine's BC3 and BC4 output to it; `README-VENDOR.md` names the first as the proof. FJ1 and FJ8 name these locations now; `search_max` (FJ8) and the corpus test `ffi_equals_reference_on_corpus` need the bench crate and stay on `bench`. The host-plugin docs (`docs/photoshop-plugin.md`, `gimp-plugin.md`, `paintdotnet-plugin.md`, wiki `Host-Plugins` and `Plugins-Compared`) left `main` with the plugins (Note FG); they stay on branch `host-plugins`.
- **FM9 Engine 2.0.0 has one encode version; a 1.x record is a stale baseline (plan task 3, review focus 1, 2026-11-02).** `ENGINE_VERSION` is 2.0.0 (the workspace version and its entries in `Cargo.lock`) and `manifest::encode_version` returns `"2.0.0"` for every sub-type from one `|` arm list, still exhaustive (a new `Subtype` fails to compile). `LEGACY_ENCODE_VERSION` is gone. `RELEASED_ENGINE_VERSION` stays `"1.1.0"` until the release task, so the guard in `encode_versions.rs` accepts every encode-input change meanwhile; `identity.json` (FM1) carries the byte proof. A manifest record without `encode_version` (schema 1 or 2, written by 1.0.0 to 1.2.0) is a known, stale baseline: `encode_current` is false for it, `IfChanged` re-encodes and replaces the output (its `output_stamp` still marks the file as ours, so no `OUT-UNKNOWN-EXISTING`), and no notice is shown for it (the re-encode report was removed, Note FM21). `ManifestEntry::from_value` stays as the overwrite guard's reader for such records (it needs `schema`, `output` and `output_stamp`; a record without `schema` or `output` is skipped as before); `MANIFEST_SCHEMA` stays 2, a record of a newer schema stays raw. `migrate_manifest_record`, `MANIFEST_STEPS` and `Manifest::load`'s call to the migrate hook are gone. `golden.rs` `two_d_outputs_unchanged` (it held 2D arms at 1.0.0) is deleted, and so are the BC4 arm list in `events_manifest.rs` and the engine-side tests of the 1.0.0 record rule; `api.rs` `a_1x_manifest_record_is_known_and_stale` (schemas 1 and 2) and `also_export.rs` `a_1x_twin_without_encode_version_is_re_encoded_...` replace them. Output bytes did not change: `identity.json` is byte-identical, and `encode_inputs.json` and `profile_hashes.json` were not rewritten.
- **FM10 Removed keys and values are errors that name the fix (`crates/engine/src/migrate.rs`, ruling P21, 2026-11-02).** `migrate::REMOVED` is a table of `Removed { key, value, hint }`; every removal task of 2.0 adds its rows. `key` is dot-separated segments compared with the last segments of each key's path, so `encoder.backend` matches under `[defaults]`, `[subtypes.X]`, `[files."k"]` and `[[rules]]` tables alike; array indices are not segments, a file key with dots is one segment, and a table key matches (`subtypes.rfaos`). `value: None` matches any value, `Some(v)` a string equal to `v` or an array holding it. A removed key is an error even when its value is harmless. `removed_in(table)` returns the first hit in document order as `<dotted path>: removed in 2.0; <hint>` (a path shows array entries as `rules[0].subtype` and quotes a key that is not a bare key); `removed_set(field, value)` does the same for one `--set` (a `file:<glob>.` prefix counts by its last segments; `subtype:<id>.<field>` checks `subtypes.<id>`), and `parse_set` calls it before its "unknown field" and "unknown sub-type" errors. `migrate_config` and `migrate_preset` check the version first (a newer file may use keys this sdc does not know, so `Newer` wins), then the removed keys (`MigrateError::Removed`). `ConfigFile::parse` maps it to `PresetError::Parse("<origin>: <message>")`, the preset loader to `<source label>: <message>`. In the GUI a project file with a removed key opens read-only with that text and is not rewritten (`crates/gui/tests/project.rs` `a_removed_key_opens_read_only_and_keeps_the_file`: `ProjectDoc::open` runs `config()` and marks any rejected config read-only, not only TOML syntax errors). The first rows are `subtypes.rfaos` (any value), `subtype = "rfaos"` and `subtypes` holding `"rfaos"` (a rule's `match.subtypes`).
- **FM11 `rfaos` is not a sub-type id any more (supersedes R-skin-1's alias, BQ5; 2026-11-02).** `Subtype::SkinRfaos` has no serde alias, `subtype_schema` and the `rfaos` entry that `schemas::subtypes_schema` added (deprecated) are gone, so the JSON schemas list only `skin_rfaos`. `[subtypes.rfaos]`, `subtype = "rfaos"` and `--set subtype=rfaos` fail with FM10's message. `DuplicateSubtypeAlias` is gone: a file naming `rfaos` fails whether or not `skin_rfaos` is also there. The packed file suffix `_rfaos` and the `packing.recipe` value `rfaos` are not sub-type ids and are unchanged.
- **FM12 The manifest-compatibility shims are gone (audit §3, 2026-11-02).** 2.0 re-encodes everything once (FM9), so manifests need not stay valid. `BakeJob::identity_sha256` always hashes the `_n`'s `resize`; its tuple keeps a literal null where the RNM settings joined (Note GW3). `PackJob::hand_placed` is removed (and from `schema/event.schema.json`): `pack::source_fill` always returns `ch:<rgba>`, the channels the source feeds, after `read:<id>` when there is one (Note DY21), so every packed source of every record names its channels. `PackSource.read` keeps its `skip_serializing_if`. Hashes and records change; output bytes do not (`identity.json` unchanged).
- **FM13 The old-version upgrade code is gone; the newer-version refusals stay (audit §3, controller ruling P4, 2026-11-02).** Deleted: `PRESET_STEPS`, `CONFIG_STEPS`, `MANIFEST_STEPS`, `run`, `manifest_v1_to_v2`, `check_aliases` (the migrate one; `PresetRegistry::check_aliases` for suffix aliases is a different check and stays). Kept: `NoVersion` and `Newer` (`PresetError::NewerSchema`, `NewerConfigSchema`), `ManifestEntry::is_newer_schema`, and `PresetRegistry.tables`. The IN-FLOAT-LDR tests stay where they are for the task that moves them.
- **FM14 NVTT is removed everywhere; VAL-BC6H-PEAK advice reworded (plan task 4, 2026-11-02).** The optional external encoder backend (`nvcompress`) is gone: `crates/engine/src/nvtt.rs`, the `nvcompress` test fake, `Backend::Nvtt`, `EncoderVersions.nvtt`, `BatchRequest.nvtt`, `--nvtt` on `convert`, the three codes `ENC-NVTT-MISSING`, `ENC-NVTT-VERSION` and `ENC-NVTT-FAILED` (118 codes become 115), the GUI's NVTT path (Advanced drawer, `gui.toml` `nvtt_path`, the picker purpose), the Encoder row in the settings, preset and bulk panels, the Encoder filter in the Textures column (and the saved `filter_encoder`), the `NVTT-Backend` wiki page and the bug template's NVTT dropdown. The README charts and the Research tables keep NVTT 3 as a reference encoder (Note HA1). A preview now always encodes with the profile's own backend. `encoder.backend = "nvtt"` and an `allow` list naming one of the three codes are errors that name the removal (`migrate::REMOVED`, FM10); `--nvtt` is a usage error (exit 2) from clap. `identity.json` is unchanged: no built-in output moved. `VAL-BC6H-PEAK` advice no longer offers NVTT as the other way to keep the peak: it says to set `encoder.effort = "slowest"`. Supersedes: Y9, all of Note NV, AF3 (and its AF9 sub-bullet), BC9, BC12, BQ3, CV3 and CV15 (moved whole); AF10, AF11, AG40, AG42, AG46, AG111, AM5, BC13, BS3, CN33, CP4, CV21, DM4, DT2, FB8, FE7, FF6, FL2 and the BC7-exactness bullet of Note AH (the NVTT wording edited out, old wording archived). Code comments that cited NV, AF3 or BC9 went with the code they described.
- **FM15 The format alone picks the encoder; the rgbcx level is fixed; the mode-6 byte patch is gone (plan task 5, 2026-11-02).** `EncoderSpec` has no `backend` or `rgbcx_level`. `Backend`, `policy::backend_fits`, `default_backend`, `backend_follows_format`, the sources that tracked a pinned backend, and the `CFG-INVALID` checks for an encoder that cannot write the format and for a level above 18 are deleted. `encode_band` already chose by `p.format`: rgbcx for BC1, BC3, BC4 and BC5, bc7e for BC7, ISPC for BC6H. `bc1_encode_block` and `bc3_encode_block` take no level and pass `RGBCX_MAX_LEVEL` (18, the value every built-in preset set). No encoded byte changes: `identity.json` is untouched, while `profile_hashes.json` and `encode_inputs.json` change because the encoder spec lost two fields.
  - **Removed names.** `migrate::REMOVED` gains `encoder.backend` (any value, after FM14's `nvtt` row, so that value still names NVTT), `encoder.rgbcx_level` and `allow` = `IN-FLOAT-LDR`. `Code::parse_allow` reads the same table, so a config, a preset, `--set` and `--allow` all give the removal message. `IN-FLOAT-LDR` leaves the catalogue (114 codes); it was never emitted.
  - **Patches are required.** `build.rs` stops the build when `bc7e_ispc.h` lacks `m_exact_opaque_alpha` (patch 0001) or `m_endpoint_search` (patch 0002). `encode_band` loses its `native` parameter, `bc7_opaque_fix` its `mode6_patch` arm, and `bc7_fixup::force_opaque_mode6` goes with its tests. `check_profile` no longer refuses `encoder.effort = "max"`. The rotated mode 4/5 fix stays. The `BC7E_*_NATIVE` constants, their asserts, the `sdc_bc7e_patched` cfgs and function and the shim's `#if` branches are gone; the `build.rs` check is the only guard.
  - **Vendor header.** `vendor/bc7enc_rdo/rgbcx_table4_small.h` is deleted. `rgbcx.cpp` names it only inside the `RGBCX_USE_SMALLER_TABLES` branch, which `rgbcx.h` defaults to 0 and nothing sets, so it is never compiled; the vendored source is left as it is. `SHA256SUMS` and `README-VENDOR.md` follow.
  - **GUI.** The Advanced drawer loses the rgbcx level row, the pin summary loses its encoder word (it read "bc7e", "rgbcx" or "ispc (BC6H)"), and the preset creator no longer moves a backend with the format. Supersedes: Owner checks pending 8 (`IN-FLOAT-LDR` block), S2 and BY1 (archived); D, S4, BZ5, CB1, CP4, CP11 and FF1 (in part). The psdds items DC5, DC8, DC10 and DC11 describe the plug-in on branch `host-plugins`, which is not changed here.
- **FM16 The `validation.*` thresholds are constants; project-wide fills are gone (plan task 6, controller rulings P10 and P13, 2026-11-02).** `ValidationSpec` keeps only `mips`; `Profile.validation` is `#[serde(default)]`, so `sdc-base.toml` no longer has a `validation` line. The other five fields are `pub const`s in `engine::validate`, with the values every preset set: `MIN_MIP_PSNR_DB` 30.0, `COVERAGE_TOLERANCE_PTS` 2.0, `COMPLEX_M_WARN_ALPHA` 247, `BC6H_MIN_PEAK_RATIO` 0.8, `PGPATCHER_NON_WHITE_SHARE` 0.5. `validate()` reads them directly; nothing else read the fields. They leave the `--set` field list. No encoded byte or validation verdict changes: `identity.json` is untouched, while `profile_hashes.json` changes because the profile lost five fields.
  - **Removed names.** `migrate::REMOVED` gains the five `validation.<key>` rows (any value; the hint gives the fixed value) and `defaults.packing.fill` (matches only under `[defaults]`, so a per-file, per-sub-type or `--set packing.fill.*` fill stays legal). The hint tells the user to delete the `[defaults]` line by hand and set fills per set, because a file the engine refuses opens read-only in the GUI and the Clear button below is gone.
  - **No engine fallback to delete.** `packing.fill.*` is an ordinary `Profile` field, merged like any other layer; nothing in the engine read `[defaults] packing.fill` apart from that merge. The removal is the `REMOVED` row alone.
  - **GUI.** The Advanced drawer loses the "Project-wide fills (legacy)" line and its Clear button (`legacy_fills`, `clear_legacy_fills`, `ClearLegacyFills`); `ProjectDoc::fill`, `set_fill` and their table helpers are deleted. No empty gap is left: the line was drawn only while a legacy fill existed.
  - Supersedes: R-fills-2 (in part: the legacy-line sentence). T5 and Y6 describe `validation.mips`, which stays; no ruling named the five thresholds as settings.
- **FM17 BC1 and the colour half of BC3 encode with rgbcx Dual; the decoder setting is gone (plan task 7, controller ruling P11, 2026-11-02).** rgbcx mode `cBC1Dual = 4` scores NVIDIA and AMD decoder error together (patch "rgbcx BC1 dual", cherry-picked from `research/bc1-dual` 9363fa72). It is the only BC1 target: BC1 outputs, the colour block of BC3, and the BC1 copies an Also export writes (a file copy of the main output) all use it. Measurements: [the Research page](../docs/wiki/Research.md).
  - **API.** `encoders_sys::init()` is infallible and idempotent (`Once`) and selects Dual; `InitError` is gone. `Bc1Model` is `{ Nvidia = 1, Amd = 2 }`, decode models only. `Session::new()` and `BatchRequest` take no model; `decode_level` and `decode_level_rgba` take none. `engine::manifest::BC1_ENCODE_ID` (`"dual"`) is written to `ManifestRecord.bc1_model` for every record and `is_up_to_date` compares it for BC1 and BC3 outputs, so a 1.x record (a decoder model's name) is stale once. `versions().rgbcx_patch` is `bc4-round-to-nearest+bc4-search+bc1-dual`; BC1, BC3, BC4 and BC5 compare it, so their records go stale once too (BC4 and BC5 bytes do not change).
  - **Decoder rule.** Validation, previews, readouts and PSNR decode BC1 with `Bc1Model::Nvidia` only, one decode so each readout has one value. Dual's NVIDIA PSNR is the lower of its two vendor values on the research corpus (39.51 against 39.74 dB). A BC1 DDS read as an input (`load.rs`, the `dds_patch` transparency scan) keeps the exact model-less `bcdec_rs` decode (controller ruling T7-a): a re-encode must not bake one vendor's decoder error into the pixels, so `ANALYSIS_VERSION` stayed 1 for it (Note FM21 bumped it to 2 for another reason).
  - **Removed.** `--bc1-decoder` (clap refuses it, exit 2), `Bc1Choice`, the GUI's BC1 decoder combo and its "restart Tessalith to apply" line, and `Session::bc1_model()`. `REMOVED` gains `bc1_model`, so a config, preset or `--set` that names it is an error that says so (`bc1_model_in_a_config_is_named`). A 1.x `gui.toml` with `bc1_decoder` still loads (serde ignores the key; `a_1x_gui_toml_with_bc1_decoder_still_loads`). Files touched under the NIF team's shared GUI paths: `crates/gui/src/model/{mod,overlay,preview}.rs` and `worker.rs` (the `Session::new()` call and the decode parameter); `ui/mod.rs` is untouched.
  - **Pin.** `crates/encoders-sys/tests/bc1_dual.rs` pins the summed NVIDIA + AMD squared error of the 64x64 research image at 4 998 895.
  - Supersedes: F (the BC1 decoder model bullet), CN32/CN33/CN64 (in part: the per-process model sentences).
- **FM18 The BC1 and BC3 identity pins moved once (plan task 7, 2026-11-02).** A separate commit before Dual pinned four more BC1/BC3 outputs with the Ideal encoder (`cov/textures/c/srgb_bc1`, `srgb_bc3` through `pbr_albedo`, `wide_bc1`, `wide_bc3` at 32x16), beside `opaque_bc1`, `blend_bc3` and `cutout_bc3`. The Dual commit re-pinned exactly those seven keys' `payload_sha256`, and no BC4, BC5, BC7 or BC6H key: `cov/textures/c/opaque_bc1.dds`, `srgb_bc1.dds`, `wide_bc1.dds`, `blend_bc3.dds`, `cutout_bc3.dds`, `srgb_bc3.dds`, `wide_bc3.dds`. `encode_inputs.json` did not change (the pins hash encoder inputs, not output bytes). Supersedes: none.
- **FM19 The BC3 alpha guard follows each texture's alpha-test cutoff (plan task 8, 2026-11-02).** The guard that keeps the BC4-style alpha search from flipping texels across the alpha test used the constant 128. It now uses the cutoff of the texture's resolved `alpha` settings, `engine::encode::alpha_cut`: `gt` t is t + 1 and `ge` t is t, so a texel passes iff alpha >= cutoff (threshold is 1..=254, so the cutoff is 1..=255). The value is already the per-mesh scan result when a mesh decides it (`mesh_alpha_policy`, provenance `mesh:alpha`), else the preset, `sdc.toml` or `--set` value, so the CLI needs no extra input and keeps 127/`gt` unless set. The default (127, `gt`) gives 128, so default output is byte-identical (`identity.json` is unchanged; `cov/textures/c/cutout_bc3_t200` is a new key at `alpha.threshold = 200`). The cutoff reaches the C++ as bits 8..15 of the `mode_flag` (0 means 128); `bc3_encode_block` takes it, and the `rgbcx_patch` tag gains `+bc4-guard-cut`, so BC1, BC3, BC4 and BC5 records go stale once. BC4 and BC5 have no alpha test and take no guard.
  - Supersedes: FJ9.
- **FM20 Opt-in BC1 1-bit alpha (plan task 9, controller rulings P8 and P10, 2026-11-02).** `encoder.bc1_alpha` (bool, default off, not serialised when off) stores BC1 with 1-bit alpha. No preset, rule, analysis or mesh sets it; the user does (GUI: **1-bit alpha** in the Alpha group of the texture panel and of the preset editor, shown only for BC1). `Profile::bc1_one_bit_alpha()` is format BC1 with the flag on.
  - **Removed name.** It replaces `encoder.bc1_transparent_black`, which `migrate::REMOVED` names with the hint "delete the line; use encoder.bc1_alpha for 1-bit alpha" (any value, so a 1.x file with `= false` must drop the line). `sdc-base.toml` loses it.
  - **Cutoff.** Every level is cut at `alpha_cut(&p.alpha)` (FM19; 128 at the default 127/`gt`) after the mips are built: alpha at or above the cutoff becomes 255, a texel below it becomes `[0, 0, 0, 0]`. The cut is in `quantize::to_encoder_input`, so encode, validation and the encode-input hash see the same image, and validation compares transparent texels as the black they decode to.
  - **Encoder.** `rgbcx::encode_bc1_alpha` (`bc1_encode_block_alpha(px, mask)`), mask bit i = texel i alpha < 128. Mask 0 gives exactly `encode_bc1`'s bytes; mask 0xFFFF gives endpoints 0, 0 with every selector 3; any other mask gives a 3-colour block fitted to the opaque texels only (principal axis, up to 8 least-squares refits, then a greedy ±1 endpoint search), opaque texels on selectors 0..2 scored with the Dual NVIDIA + AMD error (FM17), masked texels on selector 3. The `rgbcx_patch` tag gains `+bc1-alpha`.
  - **Mips (P8).** With the flag on, `cutout`, `cutout_blend` and `blend` all build coverage-preserving cutout mips (`coverage_enabled()`), then cut. `mips.coverage = "off"` turns that off for `blend` too. `VAL-COVERAGE` applies. The GUI treats `blend` with the flag as alpha-tested (`is_cutout`), so its threshold and coverage rows show.
  - **Policy.** `stores_alpha(p)` counts BC1 as storing alpha only with the flag on and policy `cutout`, `blend` or `cutout_blend`. Otherwise `FMT-BC1-ALPHA` blocks; `data` blocks too, unless its alpha is empty (FM39). With the flag on, `ALPHA-BC1-ONE-BIT` (warning; 115 codes) fires when more than 1 % (`BC1_SOFT_ALPHA_SHARE`) of the source texels have alpha 1..=254; only varying alpha is counted.
  - **Hashes (P10).** Dropping the always-serialised `bc1_transparent_black` changes every profile hash and encode input (`profile_hashes.json`, `encode_inputs.json` rewritten); every encode-version arm is already 2.0.0, so nothing re-encodes beyond 2.0's own pass. `identity.json` keeps every key and gains `cov/textures/c/cutout_bc1` (cutout, box mips, flag on).
  - Supersedes: F (in part: BC1 opaque-only), K's `bc1_transparent_black` bullet (archived), T2 (in part: "`bc1_transparent_black` stays false").
- **FM21 Fifteen info and low-value warning diagnostic codes and the re-encode report are removed (plan task 10, 2026-11-02).** No encoded byte changes: `identity.json` is untouched, `encode_inputs.json` and `profile_hashes.json` do not move. The catalogue goes from 115 to 100 codes.
  - **Codes.** `LOSS-BITDEPTH`, `CHAN-CONSTANT-ALPHA-DATA`, `CHAN-NORMAL-SHORT`, `CHAN-AO-INVERTED`, `CHAN-GLOSS-AS-ROUGH`, `ALPHA-254`, `ALPHA-NEAR-OPAQUE`, `ALPHA-PBR-NORMAL-DROPPED`, `SET-COMPANION-OUTSIDE`, `IN-ICC-IGNORED`, `PACK-ORM-OVERRIDDEN`, `RNM-AO-MISSING`, `SKIN-WET-FORM`, `DERIVE-NO-SCAN` and `DERIVE-NO-ENVMAP` leave `codes.rs`, their emit sites, the GUI sentences (`code_text.rs`) and the schemas. Each is a `migrate::REMOVED` row (`allow`, one value per row, a hint that names the code), so a config, a preset, `--set allow=` and `--allow` all fail with that message through `Code::parse_allow`.
  - **The behaviours stay; only the message goes.** Alpha 251..=254 still counts as opaque (Note L); a `data` alpha that is constant is still written as `data`; mip 0 of a short normal is still renormalised under `mips.normal_base`; a loose map still wins its slot over an `_orm` channel (`Recipe.notes` and the `prefer` helper are gone); a bent layer with no AO source still uses a uniform weight; the env `_m` is still written only for a scanned Environment Map mesh. The `skin_wet` BC4 height form is gone with the grey wet form (HB3).
  - **A PBR normal's alpha (audit trap).** Only the `add(AlphaPbrNormalDropped, ..)` call is deleted. The `st != Subtype::PbrNormal` test around `LOSS-ALPHA-DATA` stays, so a `pbr_normal` with vanilla specular alpha still raises no `LOSS-ALPHA-DATA` (P25). `near_opaque_alpha_still_resolves_opaque`, `pbr_normal_alpha_drop_is_silent_not_a_block` and the golden `near_opaque_normal_alpha_is_removed_silently` pin it; `inferred_remove_on_diffuse_and_vanilla_normal_still_block` pins the other side.
  - **`SET-COMPANION-OUTSIDE` is removed outright (controller ruling T10-a: the owner cut it).** A companion image on disk but outside the batch still skips the bake, and nothing reports it. It is not folded into `SET-MISSING-COMPANION`, which would raise it to that code's severity.
  - **Analysis fields.** Removed because only these codes read them (each checked with `git grep`): `Analysis.alpha_near_opaque`, `normal_len_mean`, `blue_mostly_black`, `red_mostly_high`, `icc_profile` (with `SourceMeta.icc_profile`, the ICC read in `load.rs` and the resource 1039 read in `psd.rs`) and `AlphaHistogram.eq254`; `AlphaStats::share_254` goes too. `AlphaStats.eq254` (decoded alpha) stays: `sdc inspect` prints its `=254` share and the validation report carries it. `ANALYSIS_VERSION` is 2 and `analysis_version.json` is re-pinned (the GUI's on-disk analysis cache is dropped once, accepted for 2.0).
  - **The re-encode report is gone (supersedes FC7 in part; FM9's notice).** `manifest::recode_causes`, `RecodeCause`, `RecodeWhy`, `sdc convert`'s stderr lines, `PlanView.recode`, `AppModel.recode`, Review's Warning rows and the "First export" line are deleted, with `crates/engine/tests/recode_causes.rs`. The behaviour is unchanged: `IfChanged` still re-encodes a record whose encode version, profile hash or bc1 id is stale, and a 1.x record is a known, stale baseline (`a_1x_manifest_record_is_known_and_stale`). FC7's `sdc validate` cube-warning and its CN3/CN4 bullets stay.
  - **GUI.** The Files-row table in `model/bottom.rs` lists `DERIVE-NIF-SUGGEST` alone. Files touched under the NIF team's shared GUI paths: `crates/gui/src/model/{bottom,code_text,items,mod,pbr_export}.rs` and `crates/gui/src/ui/review.rs`; `ui/mod.rs` and `worker.rs` are untouched.
  - Supersedes: FC7 (in part), the notice sentence of FM9, and the sentences that introduced each code: J, L, P25, R-skin-1, R1, R8, S8, W9, AC2, AC7, AH1, AL2, AL4, AL5, CN17, CN20, CN25, CN26, CN67, CV5, FC3, FC9, FC11, FC12, FF8, DM8's "amended by FC7" and FM17's `ANALYSIS_VERSION` sentence (all in part).

- **FM22 CLI extras are removed: `.dds.json` sidecars, `patch-header`, `presets diff|export|validate`, `--fill`, `sdc encoders`, `--strict-cubemaps` (plan task 11, 2026-11-02).** No encoded byte changes: `identity.json` is untouched. Removed flags and verbs give clap's own "unrecognized" error (exit 2), which names the word.
  - **Sidecars.** `BatchRequest.sidecar`, `--sidecar`, `FileTracker.sidecar_written`, the sidecar write and the removal of an old one in `process_file`, `sidecar_present` and the code `OUT-SIDECAR-KEPT` are deleted. Tessalith neither writes nor deletes a `.dds.json`: one left by 1.x stays on disk, after a re-encode and after a failed manifest append, and is never read (test `a_1x_dds_json_is_never_deleted`). The `_sdc/generated.json` file of `sdc-project.toml` (FC11, FC14, GI23) is a different file and stays.

  - **Presets.** `presets diff`, `export` and `validate` are deleted with `PresetRegistry::export`, `validate_file`, `loaded_from` and `texts` (no other caller; the GUI preset creator uses `table`, `remove`, `check_preset` and `add_file`, which stay) and the `toml_edit` dependency of `engine`. `presets list`, `show` and `schema` stay.
  - **`--fill` and `sdc encoders`.** `--set packing.fill.<role>=<value>` is the command-line form of a fill. `sdc --version` prints the engine version and then each vendored encoder's version (`encoders_sys::versions()`), one per line; the dispatched ISA stays in each manifest record.

  - **Allow lists.** `OUT-SIDECAR-KEPT`, `DDS-HEADER-PATCHED` and `DDS-HEADER-UNPATCHABLE` have `migrate::REMOVED` rows, so a config, `--set` or `--allow` that names one gets the removal message (`removed_output_codes_in_allow_lists_are_named`). `Code::ALL` goes from 100 to 97. The copied CLI command (`cli_command`) carries none of the removed flags (`copy_cli_command_uses_no_removed_flag`).
  - Supersedes: the `.dds.json` and `patch-header` parts of F (the Provenance bullet), answer 15, S (the intro and the same-file guard bullet), S7, X6, Y2, AV-S1, AZ1, BQ7, BQ10, BV3, BV4, BY9, CN33, CN42, CN63, CV2, FB1, FB8, FI3, BI6 and the FM20 Patch header bullet; T8 (note T and FC), BC5, BC7, FM13 and FM14 as far as they name `presets diff|export|validate` and `sdc encoders`; M5 and AL3 as far as they name `--fill`; J's `encoders`; DS12 and FC7's cube-check ruling (the 2D file warns in 1.x), which FM22 replaces by failing it.
- **FM23 The Also export writes no zip, sums file or conversion report; Krita and OpenRaster files are not read (plan task 12, controller rulings).** An Also export writes the PBR, Vanilla and CM folders and nothing beside them. Gone: `[export] zip`, `engine::zip`, `also::report`, `AlsoSummary::{report, zips, zip_failed, report_failed}`, `AlsoSpec::inner_layer_sets`, the Review checkbox "Also write a zip of each folder" with its Blockers row, `AppModel::set_zip_limits`, and the codes `ALSO-ZIP-FAILED` and `ALSO-REPORT-FAILED`. The key and both codes have `migrate::REMOVED` rows, so a config, a preset, `--set` or `--allow` that names one gets the removal message and not a bare serde error. The `start_also` / `finish_also` split and the GUI hand-off stay: `finish_also(run, extra, cancel)` walks each folder that ran and notes every file this run did not write (`NOT_WRITTEN`, CN94; ruling P12), the `extra` files counting as written. `derive_error` and the skipped-set `SetNote`s (`skipped_sets`) moved from `report.rs` to `also/mod.rs`. The CLI prints `AlsoSummary::notes` and `AlsoSummary::sets` after an Also export as plain lines on stderr, one per note ("not written by this export", "not derived: N maps undecided"), where the GUI lists them in its export notes. The `_sdc/also.json` record is gone with `OwnedFiles`, `OwnedWriteError`, the code `ALSO-OWNED-UNREADABLE` (a `migrate::REMOVED` row too) and the Problems row and button **Reset also.json**; an `also.json` a 1.x export left is never read, rewritten or deleted. `.kra` and `.ora` stay discovered as inputs and are `IN-DECODE` with "Krita and OpenRaster files are not read; export the image as PNG" from `load_image`, `load_image_meta` and `image_size`; `engine::archive` and its inflation caps go, and `flate2` is a dev-dependency of the engine (a test fixture). No encoded byte changes, and `identity.json` is untouched.
  - Supersedes, whole: CN45 to CN53, CN93, AC6, AF2, AF9's AF2 sub-bullet and FC12's zip-limits bullet. Whole also: CN39, CN43 and FC10's CN43 bullet. In part: CN28, CN44, CN54 to CN59, CN64, CN67, CN68, CN72, CN73, CN80, CN94, CN95, CN99, CN101, the intro of Note AC, DZ4, FA14, FC10's T8 bullet, FC12's tests bullet, GF8, GF9 and GI27.
- **FM24 Review keeps its gate; the half-size question, the warning ticks and the stale-outputs list are out (plan task 13, 2026-11-03).**
  - **The gate.** Review lists and gates on: Blockers (`export_blocks`, no export folder), Undecided textures, the Keep or Overwrite questions (an edited PGPatcher JSON, a NIF file Tessalith did not write) and the Also folders. `review_gate` is, in order: read-only `sdc.toml`, no export folder, an export running, a missing preset, a blocker, the Also gate, an unanswered overwrite question. A texture left undecided is skipped by the export, as before. An overwrite answer still stores the file's hash and is checked again at write time (CA7).
  - **Warnings are Problems rows.** `export_asks` (the PGPatcher set pitfalls, the CS PBR pitfalls) feed Problems as ordinary Warning rows (`carried`) and nothing else: no Warnings column in Review, no tick, no Acknowledge all, no `{n} warnings in Problems` link, no block. A pitfall alone does not open Review, so the header Export runs when nothing else needs an answer. A set with no match (V4) is one of these rows too. A NIF edit that cannot be written is listed in the Review footer under `NIF edits skipped (n)`, one amber `{path}: {reason}` row each, and never blocks.
  - **Half size.** There is no Review question, no `Review.half_size`, no `Measure whether full size adds anything` switch (an old `[checks] drop_top_mip` key in `gui.toml` is ignored on load) and no `LowDetailMode::Hold`, `Keep::Measure` or `Processed::UpToDate`: the engine writes a flagged texture at its planned size and `Warn` only adds the warning (`drop_top_mip` went in FM29). Encoded bytes and `identity.json` are unchanged.
  - **Stale outputs.** `engine::manifest::stale_outputs`, `StaleOutput`, the Review group `Stale outputs`, its Delete, the Problems row with `Review…`, `AppModel.stale` and `stale_json` are gone. An output the project no longer makes stays in the export folder and is left alone, as before FC11. The sidecar `_sdc/generated.json`, its reconcile on open and the hash records stay (FC11).
  - **Tests.** `review::{a_pgpatcher_pitfall_is_a_problems_row_not_a_gate, review_still_gates_on_blockers_and_overwrites, review_has_no_half_size_or_stale_group}`; the last lists every label the Review panel draws for one fixture, so a renamed or returning group fails it.
